ISAIAH — LATIN–ENGLISH (CLEMENTINE VULGATE)
Captured from the Saints.Bible staging Parallel Reader, 1 November 2026.
Editorial revisions dated 9 and 14 November 2026. The capture date above identifies the source snapshot.
Verse numbers follow the Saints (Greek/Hebrew) column; 'vulgate_reference' in verses.json gives the Vulgate's own number where it differs.

ISAIAH 1

1:1  LA: Visio Isaiæ, filii Amos, quam vidit super Judam et Jerusalem, in diebus Oziæ, Joathan, Achaz, et Ezechiæ, regum Juda.
     EN: The vision of Isaiah son of Amoz, which he saw concerning Judah and Jerusalem in the days of Uzziah, Jotham, Ahaz, and Hezekiah, kings of Judah.

1:2  LA: [Audite, cæli, et auribus percipe, terra, quoniam Dominus locutus est. Filios enutrivi, et exaltavi ; ipsi autem spreverunt me.
     EN: Hear, heavens, and listen, earth, for the Lord has spoken: I nourished children and raised them up, but they have despised Me.

1:3  LA: Cognovit bos possessorem suum, et asinus præsepe domini sui ; Israël autem me non cognovit, et populus meus non intellexit.
     EN: The ox knows its owner, and the donkey its master's manger; but Israel has not known Me, and My people have not understood.

1:4  LA: Væ genti peccatrici, populo gravi iniquitate, semini nequam, filiis sceleratis ! dereliquerunt Dominum ; blasphemaverunt Sanctum Israël ; abalienati sunt retrorsum.
     EN: Woe to the sinful nation, the people weighed down with iniquity, the wicked offspring, the criminal children! They have forsaken the Lord; they have blasphemed the Holy One of Israel; they have turned away and become estranged.

1:5  LA: Super quo percutiam vos ultra, addentes prævaricationem ? omne caput languidum, et omne cor mœrens.
     EN: Where shall I strike you further, as you add rebellion to rebellion? The whole head is sick, and the whole heart is grieving.

1:6  LA: A planta pedis usque ad verticem, non est in eo sanitas ; vulnus, et livor, et plaga tumens, non est circumligata, nec curata medicamine, neque fota oleo.
     EN: From the sole of the foot to the crown of the head there is no soundness in it: wound and bruise and swollen sore, neither bandaged nor treated with medicine nor soothed with oil.

1:7  LA: Terra vestra deserta ; civitates vestræ succensæ igni : regionem vestram coram vobis alieni devorant, et desolabitur sicut in vastitate hostili.
     EN: Your land is deserted; your cities are burned with fire. Before your eyes foreigners devour your country, and it will be desolate, as though ravaged by enemies.

1:8  LA: Et derelinquetur filia Sion ut umbraculum in vinea, et sicut tugurium in cucumerario, et sicut civitas quæ vastatur.
     EN: And daughter Zion will be left like a shelter in a vineyard, like a hut in a cucumber field, like a city laid waste.

1:9  LA: Nisi Dominus exercituum reliquisset nobis semen, quasi Sodoma fuissemus, et quasi Gomorrha similes essemus.
     EN: Had the Lord of hosts not left us offspring, we would have been like Sodom and resembled Gomorrah.

1:10  LA: Audite verbum Domini, principes Sodomorum ; percipite auribus legem Dei nostri, populus Gomorrhæ.
      EN: Hear the word of the Lord, rulers of Sodom; listen to the law of our God, people of Gomorrah.

1:11  LA: Quo mihi multitudinem victimarum vestrarum ? dicit Dominus. Plenus sum : holocausta arietum, et adipem pinguium, et sanguinem vitulorum et agnorum et hircorum, nolui.
      EN: What is the multitude of your sacrifices to Me? says the Lord. I have had My fill. I have not wanted burnt offerings of rams, the fat of fattened beasts, or the blood of calves and lambs and goats.

1:12  LA: Cum veniretis ante conspectum meum, quis quæsivit hæc de manibus vestris, ut ambularetis in atriis meis ?
      EN: When you came before My presence, who asked these things of your hands, that you should walk in My courts?

1:13  LA: Ne offeratis ultra sacrificium frustra : incensum abominatio est mihi. Neomeniam et sabbatum, et festivitates alias, non feram ; iniqui sunt cœtus vestri.
      EN: Offer no more sacrifice in vain; incense is an abomination to Me. New moon and sabbath and the other festivals I will not endure; your assemblies are wicked.

1:14  LA: Calendas vestras, et solemnitates vestras odivit anima mea : facta sunt mihi molesta ; laboravi sustinens.
      EN: My soul hates your new moons and your solemn feasts; they have become a burden to Me. I am weary of bearing them.

1:15  LA: Et cum extenderitis manus vestras, avertam oculos meos a vobis, et cum multiplicaveritis orationem, non exaudiam : manus enim vestræ sanguine plenæ sunt.
      EN: When you stretch out your hands, I will turn My eyes away from you; when you multiply your prayers, I will not hear, for your hands are full of blood.

1:16  LA: Lavamini, mundi estote ; auferte malum cogitationum vestrarum ab oculis meis : quiescite agere perverse,
      EN: Wash yourselves; be clean. Remove the evil of your thoughts from before My eyes. Cease to act perversely;

1:17  LA: discite benefacere ; quærite judicium, subvenite oppresso, judicate pupillo, defendite viduam.]
      EN: learn to do good. Seek justice; help the oppressed; judge the orphan's case; defend the widow.

1:18  LA: [Et venite, et arguite me, dicit Dominus. Si fuerint peccata vestra ut coccinum, quasi nix dealbabuntur ; et si fuerint rubra quasi vermiculus, velut lana alba erunt.
      EN: Come, then, and bring your charge against Me, says the Lord. If your sins are like scarlet, they will be made white as snow; if they are red as crimson, they will be white as wool.

1:19  LA: Si volueritis, et audieritis me, bona terræ comeditis.
      EN: If you are willing and listen to Me, you will eat the good things of the land.

1:20  LA: Quod si nolueritis, et me ad iracundiam provocaveritis, gladius devorabit vos, quia os Domini locutum est.
      EN: But if you are unwilling and provoke Me to anger, the sword will devour you, for the mouth of the Lord has spoken.

1:21  LA: Quomodo facta est meretrix civitas fidelis, plena judicii ? justitia habitavit in ea, nunc autem homicidæ.
      EN: How has the faithful city, full of justice, become a prostitute? Righteousness dwelt in her, but now murderers do.

1:22  LA: Argentum tuum versum est in scoriam ; vinum tuum mistum est aqua.
      EN: Your silver has turned to dross; your wine is mixed with water.

1:23  LA: Principes tui infideles, socii furum. Omnes diligunt munera, sequuntur retributiones. Pupillo non judicant, et causa viduæ non ingreditur ad illos.
      EN: Your rulers are faithless, companions of thieves. They all love gifts and chase rewards. They do not judge the orphan's case, and the widow's cause does not come before them.

1:24  LA: Propter hoc ait Dominus, Deus exercituum, Fortis Israël : Heu ! consolabor super hostibus meis, et vindicabor de inimicis meis.
      EN: Therefore the Lord, the God of hosts, the Mighty One of Israel, says: Ah! I will find relief against My adversaries and avenge Myself upon My enemies.

1:25  LA: Et convertam manum meam ad te, et excoquam ad puram scoriam tuam, et auferam omne stannum tuum.
      EN: I will turn My hand toward you, smelt away your dross entirely, and remove all your tin.

1:26  LA: Et restituam judices tuos ut fuerunt prius, et consiliarios tuos sicut antiquitus ; post hæc vocaberis civitas justi, urbs fidelis.
      EN: I will restore your judges as they were before, and your counselors as in ancient days. After this you will be called the city of the righteous, the faithful city.

1:27  LA: Sion in judicio redimetur, et reducent eam in justitia.
      EN: Zion will be redeemed through judgment, and they will bring her back in righteousness.

1:28  LA: Et conteret scelestos, et peccatores simul ; et qui dereliquerunt Dominum consumentur.
      EN: And He will crush criminals and sinners together, and those who have forsaken the Lord will be consumed.

1:29  LA: Confundentur enim ab idolis quibus sacrificaverunt, et erubescetis super hortis quos elegeratis,
      EN: For they will be put to shame by the idols to which they sacrificed, and you will blush over the gardens you had chosen,

1:30  LA: cum fueritis velut quercus defluentibus foliis, et velut hortus absque aqua.
      EN: when you become like an oak shedding its leaves and like a garden without water.

1:31  LA: Et erit fortitudo vestra ut favilla stuppæ, et opus vestrum quasi scintilla, et succendetur utrumque simul, et non erit qui extinguat.]
      EN: Your strength will be like the ash of tow, and your work like a spark; both will burn together, with no one to put them out.

ISAIAH 2

2:1  LA: Verbum quod vidit Isaias, filius Amos, super Juda et Jerusalem.
     EN: The word that Isaiah son of Amoz saw concerning Judah and Jerusalem.

2:2  LA: [Et erit in novissimis diebus : præparatus mons domus Domini in vertice montium, et elevabitur super colles ; et fluent ad eum omnes gentes,
     EN: In the last days, the mountain of the Lord's house will stand established at the summit of the mountains and be raised above the hills; all nations will stream to it.

2:3  LA: et ibunt populi multi, et dicent : Venite, et ascendamus ad montem Domini, et ad domum Dei Jacob ; et docebit nos vias suas, et ambulabimus in semitis ejus, quia de Sion exibit lex, et verbum Domini de Jerusalem.
     EN: Many peoples will go and say: Come, let us ascend to the mountain of the Lord, to the house of the God of Jacob. He will teach us His ways, and we will walk in His paths, for the law will go out from Zion, and the word of the Lord from Jerusalem.

2:4  LA: Et judicabit gentes, et arguet populos multos ; et conflabunt gladios suos in vomeres, et lanceas suas in falces. Non levabit gens contra gentem gladium, nec exercebuntur ultra ad prælium.
     EN: He will judge the nations and rebuke many peoples. They will beat their swords into plowshares and their spears into pruning hooks. Nation will not lift a sword against nation, nor will they train for battle any longer.

2:5  LA: Domus Jacob, venite, et ambulemus in lumine Domini.]
     EN: House of Jacob, come, and let us walk in the light of the Lord.

2:6  LA: [Projecisti enim populum tuum, domum Jacob, quia repleti sunt ut olim, et augeres habuerunt ut Philisthiim, et pueris alienis adhæserunt.
     EN: For You have rejected Your people, the house of Jacob, because they are filled as in former days, have had soothsayers like the Philistines, and have attached themselves to foreign children.

2:7  LA: Repleta est terra argento et auro, et non est finis thesaurorum ejus.
     EN: The land is filled with silver and gold, and there is no end to its treasures.

2:8  LA: Et repleta est terra ejus equis, et innumerabiles quadrigæ ejus. Et repleta est terra ejus idolis ; opus manuum suarum adoraverunt, quod fecerunt digiti eorum.
     EN: Their land is filled with horses, and their chariots are beyond number. Their land is also filled with idols; they have worshiped the work of their own hands, what their fingers have made.

2:9  LA: Et incurvavit se homo, et humiliatus est vir ; ne ergo dimittas eis.
     EN: The human being has bowed down, and the man has humbled himself; therefore do not forgive them.

2:10  LA: Ingredere in petram, et abscondere in fossa humo a facie timoris Domini, et a gloria majestatis ejus.
      EN: Enter the rock and hide in a pit in the ground from the dread of the Lord and the glory of His majesty.

2:11  LA: Oculi sublimes hominis humiliati sunt, et incurvabitur altitudo virorum ; exaltabitur autem Dominus solus in die illa.
      EN: The lofty eyes of humanity have been humbled, and men's pride will bow down; the Lord alone will be exalted on that day.

2:12  LA: Quia dies Domini exercituum super omnem superbum, et excelsum, et super omnem arrogantem, et humiliabitur ;
      EN: For the day of the Lord of hosts will come upon everyone proud and lofty, upon everyone arrogant, and they will be humbled;

2:13  LA: et super omnes cedros Libani sublimes et erectas, et super omnes quercus Basan,
      EN: upon all the tall and upright cedars of Lebanon, and upon all the oaks of Bashan;

2:14  LA: et super omnes montes excelsos, et super omnes colles elevatos,
      EN: upon all the high mountains, and upon all the elevated hills;

2:15  LA: et super omnem turrim excelsam, et super omnem murum munitum,
      EN: upon every high tower, and upon every fortified wall;

2:16  LA: et super omnes naves Tharsis, et super omne quod visu pulchrum est,
      EN: upon all the ships of Tarshish, and upon everything beautiful to behold.

2:17  LA: et incurvabitur sublimitas hominum, et humiliabitur altitudo virorum, et elevabitur Dominus solus in die illa ;
      EN: Human loftiness will bow down, and men's pride will be humbled; the Lord alone will be exalted on that day,

2:18  LA: et idola penitus conterentur ;
      EN: and the idols will be utterly crushed.

2:19  LA: et introibunt in speluncas petrarum, et in voragines terræ, a facie formidinis Domini et a gloria majestatis ejus, cum surrexerit percutere terram.
      EN: They will enter caves in the rocks and chasms in the earth, away from the dread of the Lord and the glory of His majesty, when He rises to strike the earth.

2:20  LA: In die illa projiciet homo idola argenti sui, et simulacra auri sui, quæ fecerat sibi ut adoraret, talpas et vespertiliones.
      EN: On that day a man will cast away the idols of his silver and the images of his gold that he had made for himself to worship—the moles and the bats.

2:21  LA: Et ingreditur scissuras petrarum et in cavernas saxorum, a facie formidinis Domini, et a gloria majestatis ejus, cum surrexerit percutere terram.
      EN: And he will enter clefts in the rocks and cavities in the crags, away from the dread of the Lord and the glory of His majesty, when He rises to strike the earth.

ISAIAH 3

3:1  LA: Ecce enim Dominator, Dominus exercituum, auferet a Jerusalem et a Juda validum et fortem, omne robur panis, et omne robor aquæ ;
     EN: For behold, the Sovereign, the Lord of hosts, will take away from Jerusalem and Judah the strong and the mighty, every support of bread and every support of water;

3:2  LA: fortem, et virum bellatorem, judicem, et prophetam, et ariolum, et senem ;
     EN: the mighty man and the warrior, the judge and the prophet, the diviner and the elder;

3:3  LA: principem super quinquaginta, et honorabilem vultu et consiliarium, et sapientem de architectis, et prudentem eloquii mystici.
     EN: the captain of fifty, the man honorable in countenance, the counselor, the skilled architect, and the one versed in mysterious speech.

3:4  LA: Et dabo pueros principes eorum, et effeminati dominabuntur eis ;
     EN: I will give them boys as rulers, and effeminate men will rule over them.

3:5  LA: et irruet populus, vir ad virum, et unusquisque ad proximum suum ; tumultuabitur puer contra senem, et ignobilis contra nobilem.
     EN: The people will rush upon one another, each upon his neighbor; the boy will rise up against the old man, and the lowborn against the noble.

3:6  LA: Apprehendet enim vir fratrem suum, domesticum patris sui : Vestimentum tibi est, princeps esto noster, ruina autem hæc sub manu tua.
     EN: For a man will seize his brother, a member of his father's household: You have a garment; be our ruler, and take this ruin into your hands.

3:7  LA: Respondebit in die illa, dicens : Non sum medicus, et in domo mea non est panis neque vestimentum : nolite constituere me principem populi.
     EN: On that day he will answer: I am no healer, and in my house there is neither bread nor clothing. Do not make me ruler of the people.

3:8  LA: Ruit enim Jerusalem, et Judas concidit, quia lingua eorum et adinventiones eorum contra Dominum, ut provocarent oculos majestatis ejus.
     EN: For Jerusalem has fallen, and Judah has collapsed, because their tongue and their schemes are against the Lord, provoking the eyes of His majesty.

3:9  LA: Agnitio vultus eorum respondit eis ; et peccatum suum quasi Sodoma prædicaverunt, nec absconderunt. Væ animæ eorum, quoniam reddita sunt eis mala !
     EN: The expression of their faces has answered them, and they have proclaimed their sin like Sodom, without hiding it. Woe to their souls, for evils have been repaid to them!

3:10  LA: Dicite justo quoniam bene, quoniam fructum adinventionum suarum comedet.
      EN: Tell the righteous that it will be well, for he will eat the fruit of his deeds.

3:11  LA: Væ impio in malum ! retributio enim manuum ejus fiet ei.
      EN: Woe to the wicked, to his hurt! For the work of his hands will be repaid to him.

3:12  LA: Populum meum exactores sui spoliaverunt, et mulieres dominatæ sunt eis. Popule meus, qui te beatum dicunt, ipsi te decipiunt, et viam gressuum tuorum dissipant.
      EN: Their own oppressors have stripped My people, and women have ruled over them. My people, those who call you blessed are the very ones who deceive you and destroy the path of your steps.

3:13  LA: Stat ad judicandum Dominus, et stat ad judicandos populos.
      EN: The Lord stands to judge; He stands to judge the peoples.

3:14  LA: Dominus ad judicium veniet cum senibus populi sui, et principibus ejus ; vos enim depasti estis vineam, et rapina pauperis in domo vestra.
      EN: The Lord will enter into judgment with the elders of His people and their rulers: For you have grazed the vineyard bare, and what was plundered from the poor is in your house.

3:15  LA: Quare atteritis populum meum, et facies pauperum commolitis ? dicit Dominus Deus exercituum.]
      EN: Why do you crush My people and grind down the faces of the poor? says the Lord, the God of hosts.

3:16  LA: [Et dixit Dominus : Pro eo quod elevatæ sunt filiæ Sion, et ambulaverunt extento collo, et nutibus oculorum ibant, et plaudebant, ambulabant pedibus suis, et composito gradu incedebant ;
      EN: And the Lord said: Because the daughters of Zion have become proud and have walked with outstretched necks, going about with beckoning eyes, clapping as they walked on their feet, and proceeding with measured steps,

3:17  LA: decalvabit Dominus verticem filiarum Sion, et Dominus crinem earum nudabit.
      EN: the Lord will make the crowns of the daughters of Zion bald, and the Lord will strip away their hair.

3:18  LA: In die illa auferet Dominus ornamentum calceamentum,
      EN: On that day the Lord will take away the adornment of their shoes,

3:19  LA: et lunulas, et torques, et monilia, et armillas, et mitras,
      EN: the little crescents, the neck chains, the necklaces, the bracelets, the headdresses,

3:20  LA: et discriminalia, et periscelidas, et murenulas, et olfactoriola, et inaures,
      EN: the hair ornaments, the anklets, the small chains, the scent bottles, the earrings,

3:21  LA: et annulos, et gemmas in fronte pendentes,
      EN: the rings, the jewels hanging on their foreheads,

3:22  LA: et mutatoria, et palliola, et linteamina, et acus,
      EN: the changes of clothing, the little cloaks, the linen garments, the pins,

3:23  LA: et specula, et sindones, et vittas, et theristra.
      EN: the mirrors, the fine linen, the ribbons, and the summer veils.

3:24  LA: Et erit pro suavi odore fœtor, et pro zona funiculus, et pro crispanti crine calvitium, et pro fascia pectorali cilicium.
      EN: Instead of sweet fragrance there will be a stench, instead of a girdle a rope, instead of curled hair baldness, and instead of a breastband sackcloth.

3:25  LA: Pulcherrimi quoque viri tui gladio cadent, et fortes tui in prælio.
      EN: Your most handsome men will also fall by the sword, and your mighty men in battle.

3:26  LA: Et mœrebunt atque lugebunt portæ ejus, et desolata in terra sedebit.]
      EN: Her gates will mourn and lament, and she will sit desolate on the ground.

ISAIAH 4

4:1  LA: Et apprehendent septem mulieres virum unum in die illa, dicentes : Panem nostrum comedemus, et vestimentis nostris operiemur : tantummodo invocetur nomen tuum super nos ; aufer opprobrium nostrum.]
     EN: On that day seven women will seize one man, saying: We will eat our own bread and clothe ourselves in our own garments; only let your name be called over us. Take away our disgrace.

4:2  LA: [In die illa, erit germen Domini in magnificentia et gloria, et fructus terræ sublimis, et exsultatio his qui salvati fuerint de Israël.
     EN: On that day the shoot of the Lord will have splendor and glory, and the fruit of the land will be lofty, a source of exultation for those of Israel who are saved.

4:3  LA: Et erit : omnis qui relictus fuerit in Sion, et residuus in Jerusalem, Sanctus vocabitur, omnis qui scriptus est in vita in Jerusalem.
     EN: And this will happen: everyone left in Zion and remaining in Jerusalem will be called holy, everyone written down for life in Jerusalem,

4:4  LA: Si abluerit Dominus sordes filiarum Sion, et sanguinem Jerusalem laverit de medio ejus, in spiritu judicii, et spiritu ardoris.
     EN: if the Lord washes away the filth of the daughters of Zion and cleanses the blood of Jerusalem from her midst by a spirit of judgment and a spirit of burning.

4:5  LA: Et creabit Dominus super omnem locum montis Sion, et ubi invocatus est, nubem per diem et fumum, et splendorem ignis flammantis in nocte : super omnem enim gloriam protectio.
     EN: The Lord will create over every place on Mount Zion, and wherever He has been invoked, a cloud and smoke by day, and the brightness of flaming fire by night; for over all the glory there will be protection.

4:6  LA: Et tabernaculum erit in umbraculum, diei ab æstu, et in securitatem et absconsionem a turbine et a pluvia.]
     EN: There will be a shelter to give shade from the day's heat, and safety and concealment from the whirlwind and the rain.

ISAIAH 5

5:1  LA: Cantabo dilecto meo canticum patruelis mei vineæ suæ. Vinea facta est dilecto meo in cornu filio olei.
     EN: I will sing to my beloved my kinsman's song about his vineyard. My beloved had a vineyard on a hill rich in oil.

5:2  LA: Et sepivit eam, et lapides elegit ex illa, et plantavit eam electam ; et ædificavit turrim in medio ejus, et torcular exstruxit in ea ; et exspectavit ut faceret uvas, et fecit labruscas.
     EN: He fenced it, cleared its stones, and planted it with choice vines. He built a tower in its midst and constructed a winepress in it. He expected it to produce grapes, but it produced wild grapes.

5:3  LA: Nunc ergo, habitatores Jerusalem et viri Juda, judicate inter me et vineam meam.
     EN: Now, inhabitants of Jerusalem and men of Judah, judge between me and my vineyard.

5:4  LA: Quid est quod debui ultra facere vineæ meæ, et non feci ei ? an quod exspectavi ut faceret uvas, et fecit labruscas ?
     EN: What more ought I to have done for my vineyard that I have not done for it? Was it that I expected it to produce grapes, and it produced wild grapes?

5:5  LA: Et nunc ostendam vobis quid ego faciam vineæ meæ : auferam sepem ejus, et erit in direptionem ; diruam maceriam ejus, et erit in conculcationem.
     EN: Now I will show you what I will do to my vineyard: I will remove its hedge, and it will be plundered; I will break down its wall, and it will be trampled.

5:6  LA: Et ponam eam desertam ; non putabitur et non fodietur : et ascendent vepres et spinæ, et nubibus mandabo ne pluant super eam imbrem.
     EN: I will make it a wasteland. It will be neither pruned nor dug, and briers and thorns will grow up. I will command the clouds not to rain upon it.

5:7  LA: Vinea enim Domini exercituum domus Israël est ; et vir Juda germen ejus delectabile : et exspectavi ut faceret judicium, et ecce iniquitas ; et justitiam, et ecce clamor.]
     EN: For the vineyard of the Lord of hosts is the house of Israel, and the man of Judah is His delightful planting. I expected it to produce judgment, and behold, iniquity; righteousness, and behold, an outcry.

5:8  LA: [Væ qui conjungitis domum ad domum, et agrum agro copulatis usque ad terminum loci ! Numquid habitabitis vos soli in medio terræ ?
     EN: Woe to you who join house to house and add field to field to the very limits of the place! Will you alone live in the midst of the land?

5:9  LA: In auribus meis sunt hæc, dicit Dominus exercituum ; nisi domus multæ desertæ fuerint, grandes et pulchræ, absque habitatore.
     EN: These things are in My ears, says the Lord of hosts: surely many houses will become deserted, great and beautiful houses without an inhabitant.

5:10  LA: Decem enim jugera vinearum facient lagunculam unam, et triginta modii sementis facient modios tres.
      EN: For ten jugera of vineyards will yield one small jar, and thirty modii of seed will yield three modii.

5:11  LA: Væ qui consurgitis mane ad ebrietatem sectandam, et potandum usque ad vesperam, ut vino æstuetis !
      EN: Woe to you who rise early to pursue drunkenness and keep drinking until evening, until you burn with wine!

5:12  LA: Cithara, et lyra, et tympanum, et tibia, et vinum in conviviis vestris ; et opus Domini non respicitis, nec opera manuum ejus consideratis.
      EN: Harp and lyre, tambourine and flute, and wine are at your banquets, but you do not look at the Lord's work or consider the works of His hands.

5:13  LA: Propterea captivus ductus est populus meus, quia non habuit scientiam, et nobiles ejus interierunt fame, et multitudo ejus siti exaruit.
      EN: Therefore My people have been led away captive because they lacked knowledge; their nobles have perished of hunger, and their multitude has dried up with thirst.

5:14  LA: Propterea dilatavit infernus animam suam, et aperuit os suum absque ullo termino ; et descendent fortes ejus, et populus ejus, et sublimes gloriosique ejus, ad eum.
      EN: Therefore the underworld has enlarged its appetite and opened its mouth without limit; their mighty men, their people, and their lofty and glorious ones will descend into it.

5:15  LA: Et incurvabitur homo, et humiliabitur vir, et oculi sublimium deprimentur.
      EN: The human being will bow down, and the man will be humbled, and the eyes of the lofty will be brought low.

5:16  LA: Et exaltabitur Dominus exercituum in judicio ; et Deus sanctus sanctificabitur in justitia.
      EN: The Lord of hosts will be exalted through judgment, and the holy God will be shown holy through righteousness.

5:17  LA: Et pascentur agni juxta ordinem suum, et deserta in ubertatem versa advenæ comedent.]
      EN: Lambs will graze in their accustomed order, and strangers will eat from deserted places restored to fertility.

5:18  LA: [Væ qui trahitis iniquitatem in funiculis vanitatis, et quasi vinculum plaustri peccatum !
      EN: Woe to you who drag iniquity with cords of vanity and sin as though with a cart rope!

5:19  LA: qui dicitis : Festinet, et cito veniat opus ejus, ut videamus ; et appropiet, et veniat consilium sancti Israël, et sciemus illud !
      EN: You say: Let Him hasten, and let His work come quickly, so that we may see it! Let the plan of the Holy One of Israel draw near and come, so that we may know it!

5:20  LA: Væ qui dicitis malum bonum, et bonum malum ; ponentes tenebras lucem, et lucem tenebras ; ponentes amarum in dulce, et dulce in amarum !
      EN: Woe to you who call evil good and good evil, who make darkness light and light darkness, who make bitter sweet and sweet bitter!

5:21  LA: Væ qui sapientes estis in oculis vestris, et coram vobismetipsis prudentes.
      EN: Woe to you who are wise in your own eyes and prudent in your own sight!

5:22  LA: Væ qui potentes estis ad bibendum vinum, et viri fortes ad miscendam ebrietatem !
      EN: Woe to you who are mighty at drinking wine and strong men at mixing intoxicating drink!

5:23  LA: qui justificatis impium pro muneribus, et justitiam justi aufertis ab eo !
      EN: You acquit the wicked for gifts and take away the righteousness of the righteous from him!

5:24  LA: Propter hoc, sicut devorat stipulam lingua ignis, et calor flammæ exurit, sic radix eorum quasi favilla erit, et germen eorum ut pulvis ascendet ; abjecerunt enim legem Domini exercituum, et eloquium sancti Israël blasphemaverunt.
      EN: Therefore, as a tongue of fire devours stubble and the heat of a flame burns it up, so their root will be like ash and their shoot will rise like dust. For they have rejected the law of the Lord of hosts and blasphemed the word of the Holy One of Israel.

5:25  LA: Ideo iratus est furor Domini in populum suum, et extendit manum suam super eum, et percussit eum : et conturbati sunt montes, et facta sunt morticina eorum quasi stercus in medio platearum. In his omnibus non est adversus furor ejus, sed adhuc manus ejus extenta.
      EN: Therefore the Lord's fury has blazed against His people; He has stretched out His hand over them and struck them. The mountains were shaken, and their corpses became like dung in the streets. Through all this His fury has not turned away, and His hand is still outstretched.

5:26  LA: Et elevabit signum in nationibus procul, et sibilabit ad eum de finibus terræ : et ecce festinus velociter veniet.
      EN: He will raise a signal among distant nations and whistle to one from the ends of the earth; behold, it will come swiftly and with haste.

5:27  LA: Non est deficiens neque laborans in eo ; non dormitabit, neque dormiet ; neque solvetur cingulum renum ejus, nec rumpetur corrigia calceamenti ejus.
      EN: No one in it is failing or weary; it will neither slumber nor sleep. The belt at its waist will not come loose, nor will the strap of its shoe break.

5:28  LA: Sagittæ ejus acutæ, et omnes arcus ejus extenti. Ungulæ equorum ejus ut silex, et rotæ ejus quasi impetus tempestatis.
      EN: Its arrows are sharp, and all its bows are drawn. Its horses' hooves are like flint, and its wheels like the rush of a storm.

5:29  LA: Rugitus ejus ut leonis ; rugiet ut catuli leonum : et frendet, et tenebit prædam, et amplexabitur, et non erit qui eruat.
      EN: Its roaring is like a lion's; it will roar like young lions. It will growl and seize the prey and hold it fast, and no one will rescue it.

5:30  LA: Et sonabit super eum in die illa sicut sonitus maris : aspiciemus in terram, et ecce tenebræ tribulationis, et lux obtenebrata est in caligine ejus.]
      EN: On that day it will thunder over him like the sound of the sea. We will look at the land, and behold, the darkness of distress; even the light has grown dark in its gloom.

ISAIAH 6

6:1  LA: In anno quo mortuus est rex Ozias, vidi Dominum sedentem super solium excelsum et elevatum ; et ea quæ sub ipso erant replebant templum.
     EN: In the year when King Uzziah died, I saw the Lord sitting upon a high and exalted throne, and what lay beneath Him filled the temple.

6:2  LA: Seraphim stabant super illud : sex alæ uni, et sex alæ alteri ; duabus velabant faciem ejus, et duabus velabant pedes ejus, et duabus volabant.
     EN: Seraphim stood above it. Each had six wings: with two they covered his face, with two they covered his feet, and with two they flew.

6:3  LA: Et clamabant alter ad alterum, et dicebant : [Sanctus, sanctus, sanctus Dominus, Deus exercituum ; plena est omnis terra gloria ejus.]
     EN: They cried to one another and said: Holy, holy, holy is the Lord, the God of hosts; the whole earth is full of His glory.

6:4  LA: Et commota sunt superliminaria cardinum a voce clamantis, et domus repleta est fumo.
     EN: The lintels over the door pivots shook at the voice of the one crying, and the house was filled with smoke.

6:5  LA: Et dixi : [Væ mihi, quia tacui, quia vir pollutus labiis ego sum, et in medio populi polluta labia habentis ego habito, et regem Dominum exercituum vidi oculis meis.]
     EN: And I said: Woe to me, for I have been silent! For I am a man of polluted lips, and I live among a people with polluted lips, and my eyes have seen the King, the Lord of hosts.

6:6  LA: Et volavit ad me unus de seraphim, et in manu ejus calculus, quem forcipe tulerat de altari,
     EN: One of the seraphim flew to me, holding in his hand a glowing stone that he had taken from the altar with tongs.

6:7  LA: et tetigit os meum, et dixit : [Ecce tetigit hoc labia tua, et auferetur iniquitas tua, et peccatum tuum mundabitur.]
     EN: He touched my mouth and said: Behold, this has touched your lips; your iniquity will be removed, and your sin will be cleansed.

6:8  LA: Et audivi vocem Domini dicentis : [Quem mittam ? et quis ibit nobis ?] Et dixi : [Ecce ego, mitte me.]
     EN: I heard the voice of the Lord saying: Whom shall I send, and who will go for Us? And I said: Here I am; send me.

6:9  LA: Et dixit : [Vade, et dices populo huic : Audite audientes, et nolite intelligere ; et videte visionem, et nolite cognoscere.
     EN: He said: Go, and say to this people: Keep hearing, but do not understand; see the vision, but do not recognize it.

6:10  LA: Excæca cor populi hujus, et aures ejus aggrava, et oculos ejus claude : ne forte videat oculis suis, et auribus suis audiat, et corde suo intelligat, et convertatur, et sanem eum.]
      EN: Blind the heart of this people, make their ears heavy, and close their eyes, lest they see with their eyes and hear with their ears and understand with their heart and turn, and I heal them.

6:11  LA: Et dixi : [Usquequo, Domine ?] Et dixit : [Donec desolentur civitates absque habitatore, et domus sine homine, et terra relinquetur deserta.
      EN: And I said: How long, Lord? He said: Until cities are laid waste without an inhabitant, houses without a person, and the land is left deserted.

6:12  LA: Et longe faciet Dominus homines, et multiplicabitur quæ derelicta fuerat in medio terræ.
      EN: The Lord will send people far away, and what had been abandoned in the midst of the land will be multiplied.

6:13  LA: Et adhuc in ea decimatio, et convertetur, et erit in ostensionem sicut terebinthus, et sicut quercus quæ expandit ramos suos ; semen sanctum erit id quod steterit in ea.]
      EN: And still a tenth will remain in it, and it will return and be put on display like a terebinth and like an oak that spreads its branches; a holy seed will be what remains standing in it.

ISAIAH 7

7:1  LA: Et factum est in diebus Achaz, filii Joathan, filii Oziæ, regis Juda, ascendit Rasin, rex Syriæ, et Phacee, filius Romeliæ, rex Israël, in Jerusalem, ad præliandum contra eam : et non potuerunt
     EN: In the days of Ahaz son of Jotham, son of Uzziah, king of Judah, Rezin king of Syria and Pekah son of Remaliah, king of Israel, went up to Jerusalem to fight against it, but they could not

7:2  LA: debellare eam. Et nuntiaverunt domui David, dicentes : Requievit Syria super Ephraim. Et commotum est cor ejus, et cor populi ejus, sicut moventur ligna silvarum a facie venti.
     EN: conquer it. The house of David was told: Syria has settled upon Ephraim. His heart and the heart of his people trembled as the trees of the forests tremble before the wind.

7:3  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad Isaiam : Egredere in occursum Achaz, tu et qui derelictus est Jasub, filius tuus, ad extremum aquæductus piscinæ superioris in via agri Fullonis ;
     EN: The Lord said to Isaiah: Go out to meet Ahaz, you and your son Jashub, the one who is left, at the end of the aqueduct of the upper pool, on the road to the Fuller's Field.

7:4  LA: et dices ad eum : [Vide ut sileas ; noli timere, et cor tuum ne formidet a duabus caudis titionum fumigantium istorum, in ira furoris Rasin, regis Syriæ, et filii Romeliæ ;
     EN: Say to him: See that you remain quiet. Do not be afraid, and do not let your heart dread these two tails of smoking firebrands, the furious anger of Rezin king of Syria and of the son of Remaliah.

7:5  LA: eo quod consilium inierit contra te Syria in malum, Ephraim, et filius Romeliæ, dicentes :
     EN: For Syria has planned evil against you with Ephraim and the son of Remaliah, saying:

7:6  LA: Ascendamus ad Judam, et suscitemus eum, et avellamus eum ad nos, et ponamus regem in medio ejus filium Tabeel.
     EN: Let us go up against Judah, stir it up, and tear it away to ourselves, and let us set up the son of Tabeel as king in its midst.

7:7  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Non stabit, et non erit istud ;
     EN: Thus says the Lord God: This will not stand, and it will not happen.

7:8  LA: sed caput Syriæ Damascus, et caput Damasci Rasin ; et adhuc sexaginta et quinque anni, et desinet Ephraim esse populus ;
     EN: But the head of Syria is Damascus, and the head of Damascus is Rezin; within sixty-five years Ephraim will cease to be a people.

7:9  LA: et caput Ephraim Samaria, et caput Samariæ filius Romeliæ. Si non credideritis, non permanebitis.]
     EN: The head of Ephraim is Samaria, and the head of Samaria is the son of Remaliah. If you do not believe, you will not endure.

7:10  LA: Et adjecit Dominus loqui ad Achaz, dicens :
      EN: The Lord spoke again to Ahaz, saying:

7:11  LA: Pete tibi signum a Domino Deo tuo, in profundum inferni, sive in excelsum supra.
      EN: Ask for yourself a sign from the Lord your God, in the depth of the underworld or in the height above.

7:12  LA: Et dixit Achaz : Non petam, et non tentabo Dominum.
      EN: But Ahaz said: I will not ask, and I will not put the Lord to the test.

7:13  LA: Et dixit : [Audite ergo, domus David. Numquid parum vobis est molestos esse hominibus, quia molesti estis et Deo meo ?
      EN: And he said: Hear, then, house of David. Is it too little for you to weary people, that you also weary my God?

7:14  LA: Propter hoc dabit Dominus ipse vobis signum : ecce virgo concipiet, et pariet filium, et vocabitur nomen ejus Emmanuel.
      EN: Therefore the Lord Himself will give you a sign: behold, a virgin will conceive and bear a son, and his name will be called Emmanuel.

7:15  LA: Butyrum et mel comedet, ut sciat reprobare malum, et eligere bonum.
      EN: He will eat butter and honey, so that he may know how to reject evil and choose good.

7:16  LA: Quia antequam sciat puer reprobare malum et eligere bonum, derelinquetur terra quam tu detestaris a facie duorum regum suorum.
      EN: For before the child knows how to reject evil and choose good, the land that you detest will be abandoned by its two kings.

7:17  LA: Adducet Dominus super te, et super populum tuum, et super domum patris tui, dies qui non venerunt a diebus separationis Ephraim a Juda, cum rege Assyriorum.
      EN: The Lord will bring upon you, your people, and your father's house days that have not come since the days when Ephraim separated from Judah, with the king of the Assyrians.

7:18  LA: Et erit in die illa : sibilabit Dominus muscæ quæ est in extremo fluminum Ægypti, et api quæ est in terra Assur ;
      EN: On that day the Lord will whistle for the fly at the farthest reaches of Egypt's rivers and for the bee in the land of Assyria.

7:19  LA: et venient, et requiescent omnes in torrentibus vallium, et in cavernis petrarum, et in omnibus frutetis, et in universis foraminibus.
      EN: They will all come and settle in the torrents of the valleys, in the caves of the rocks, in every thicket, and in every hollow.

7:20  LA: In die illa radet Dominus in novacula conducta in his qui trans flumen sunt, in rege Assyriorum, caput et pilos pedum, et barbam universam.
      EN: On that day the Lord will shave with a hired razor—with those beyond the river, with the king of the Assyrians—the head, the hair of the feet, and the entire beard.

7:21  LA: Et erit in die illa : nutriet homo vaccam boum, et duas oves,
      EN: On that day a man will rear one cow from the herd and two sheep,

7:22  LA: et præ ubertate lactis comedet butyrum ; butyrum enim et mel manducabit omnis qui relictus fuerit in medio terræ.
      EN: and from the abundance of milk he will eat butter; for everyone left in the midst of the land will eat butter and honey.

7:23  LA: Et erit in die illa : omnis locus ubi fuerint mille vites, mille argenteis, in spinas et in vepres erunt.
      EN: On that day every place where there were a thousand vines worth a thousand silver pieces will become thorns and briers.

7:24  LA: Cum sagittis et arcu ingredientur illuc : vepres enim et spinæ erunt in universa terra.
      EN: People will enter there with arrows and bow, for briers and thorns will cover the whole land.

7:25  LA: Et omnes montes qui in sarculo sarrientur, non veniet illuc terror spinarum et veprium : et erit in pascua bovis, et in conculcationem pecoris.]
      EN: As for all the mountains that are hoed with a hoe, the fear of thorns and briers will not come there; they will become pasture for oxen and ground trampled by livestock.

ISAIAH 8

8:1  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : Sume tibi librum grandem, et scribe in eo stylo hominis : Velociter spolia detrahe, cito prædare.
     EN: The Lord said to me: Take a large scroll and write in it with a man's pen: Strip the spoils quickly; plunder swiftly.

8:2  LA: Et adhibui mihi testes fideles, Uriam sacerdotem, et Zachariam, filium Barachiæ :
     EN: And I took trustworthy witnesses for myself, Uriah the priest and Zechariah son of Jeberechiah.

8:3  LA: et accessi ad prophetissam, et concepit, et peperit filium. Et dixit Dominus ad me : Voca nomen ejus : Accelera spolia detrahere ; Festina prædari :
     EN: I approached the prophetess, and she conceived and bore a son. The Lord said to me: Give him the name: Hasten to strip the spoils; hurry to plunder.

8:4  LA: quia antequam sciat puer vocare patrem suum et matrem suam, auferetur fortitudo Damasci, et spolia Samariæ, coram rege Assyriorum.
     EN: For before the child knows how to call his father and his mother, the strength of Damascus and the spoils of Samaria will be carried away before the king of the Assyrians.

8:5  LA: Et adjecit Dominus loqui ad me adhuc, dicens :
     EN: The Lord spoke to me yet again, saying:

8:6  LA: [Pro eo quod abjecit populus iste aquas Siloë, quæ vadunt cum silentio, et assumpsit magis Rasin, et filium Romeliæ :
     EN: Because this people has rejected the waters of Shiloah that flow quietly and has preferred Rezin and the son of Remaliah,

8:7  LA: propter hoc ecce Dominus adducet super eos aquas fluminis fortes et multas, regem Assyriorum, et omnem gloriam ejus, et ascendet super omnes rivos ejus, et fluet super universas ripas ejus ;
     EN: therefore, behold, the Lord will bring upon them the mighty and abundant waters of the river—the king of the Assyrians and all his glory. It will rise above all its channels and overflow all its banks;

8:8  LA: et ibit per Judam, inundans, et transiens : usque ad collum veniet. Et erit extensio alarum ejus implens latitudinem terræ tuæ, o Emmanuel !
     EN: it will pass through Judah, flooding and sweeping through, reaching as far as the neck. The spread of its wings will fill the breadth of your land, O Emmanuel!

8:9  LA: Congregamini, populi, et vincimini ; et audite, universæ procul terræ : confortamini, et vincimini ; accingite vos, et vincimini.
     EN: Gather together, peoples, and be defeated! Listen, all you distant lands. Make yourselves strong, and be defeated; gird yourselves, and be defeated.

8:10  LA: Inite consilium, et dissipabitur ; loquimini verbum, et non fiet : quia nobiscum Deus.
      EN: Form a plan, and it will be scattered; speak a word, and it will not come to pass, for God is with us.

8:11  LA: Hæc enim ait Dominus ad me : Sicut in manu forti erudivit me, ne irem in via populi hujus, dicens :
      EN: For thus the Lord said to me, as He instructed me with a strong hand not to walk in the way of this people:

8:12  LA: Non dicatis : Conjuratio ; omnia enim quæ loquitur populus iste, conjuratio est : et timorem ejus ne timeatis, neque paveatis.
      EN: Do not say, A conspiracy! For everything this people says is a conspiracy. Do not fear what it fears or be terrified.

8:13  LA: Dominum exercituum ipsum sanctificate ; ipse pavor vester, et ipse terror vester :
      EN: Sanctify the Lord of hosts Himself; let Him be your dread and Him your terror.

8:14  LA: et erit vobis in sanctificationem ; in lapidem autem offensionis, et in petram scandali, duabus domibus Israël ; in laqueum et in ruinam habitantibus Jerusalem.
      EN: He will be a sanctification for you, but a stone of stumbling and a rock of offense to the two houses of Israel, a snare and a ruin for the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

8:15  LA: Et offendent ex eis plurimi, et cadent, et conterentur, et irretientur, et capientur.
      EN: Many of them will stumble and fall and be crushed; they will be entangled and captured.

8:16  LA: Liga testimonium, signa legem in discipulis meis.
      EN: Bind up the testimony; seal the law among My disciples.

8:17  LA: Et exspectabo Dominum qui abscondit faciem suam a domo Jacob, et præstolabor eum.
      EN: I will wait for the Lord, who hides His face from the house of Jacob, and I will look for Him.

8:18  LA: Ecce ego et pueri mei quos dedit mihi Dominus in signum, et in portentum Israël a Domino exercituum, qui habitat in monte Sion :
      EN: Behold, I and my children, whom the Lord has given me, are a sign and a portent for Israel from the Lord of hosts, who dwells on Mount Zion.

8:19  LA: et cum dixerint ad vos : Quærite a pythonibus et a divinis qui strident in incantationibus suis : numquid non populus a Deo suo requiret, pro vivis a mortuis ?
      EN: And when they say to you: Consult the mediums and the diviners who chirp in their incantations—should a people not consult its God? Should it consult the dead on behalf of the living?

8:20  LA: ad legem magis et ad testimonium. Quod si non dixerint juxta verbum hoc, non erit eis matutina lux.
      EN: Turn instead to the law and to the testimony. If they do not speak according to this word, there will be no morning light for them.

8:21  LA: Et transibit per eam, corruet, et esuriet ; et cum esurierit, irascetur. Et maledicet regi suo, et Deo suo, et suscipiet sursum,
      EN: He will pass through the land, collapse, and hunger; when he is hungry, he will become angry. He will curse his king and his God, and look upward,

8:22  LA: et ad terram intuebitur ; et ecce tribulatio et tenebræ, dissolutio et angustia, et caligo persequens, et non poterit avolare de angustia sua.]
      EN: and gaze at the earth; behold, distress and darkness, dissolution and anguish, and pursuing gloom. He will not be able to fly free from his anguish.

8:23  LA: Primo tempore alleviata est terra Zabulon et terra Nephthali : et novissimo aggravata est via maris trans Jordanem Galilææ gentium.
      EN: In the former time the land of Zebulun and the land of Naphtali were made light; in the latter time the way of the sea beyond the Jordan, Galilee of the nations, was made heavy.

ISAIAH 9

9:1  LA: Populus qui ambulabat in tenebris, vidit lucem magnam ; habitantibus in regione umbræ mortis, lux orta est eis.
     EN: The people walking in darkness have seen a great light; for those dwelling in the region of the shadow of death, light has risen upon them.

9:2  LA: Multiplicasti gentem, et non magnificasti lætitiam. Lætabuntur coram te, sicut qui lætantur in messe ; sicut exsultant victores capta præda, quando dividunt spolia.
     EN: You have multiplied the nation, but You have not increased its joy. They will rejoice before You as people rejoice at harvest, as victors exult over captured plunder when they divide the spoils.

9:3  LA: Jugum enim oneris ejus, et virgam humeri ejus, et sceptrum exactoris ejus superasti, sicut in die Madian.
     EN: For You have overcome the yoke of its burden, the rod upon its shoulder, and the staff of its oppressor, as on the day of Midian.

9:4  LA: Quia omnis violentia prædatio cum tumultu, et vestimentum mistum sanguine, erit in combustionem, et cibus ignis.
     EN: For every violent plundering with tumult, and every garment mingled with blood, will be burned and become food for fire.

9:5  LA: Parvulus enim natus est nobis, et filius datus est nobis, et factus est principatus super humerum ejus : et vocabitur nomen ejus, Admirabilis, Consiliarius, Deus, Fortis, Pater futuri sæculi, Princeps pacis.
     EN: For a little child is born to us, and a son is given to us; authority has been placed upon his shoulder. His name will be called Wonderful, Counselor, God, Mighty, Father of the age to come, Prince of peace.

9:6  LA: Multiplicabitur ejus imperium, et pacis non erit finis ; super solium David, et super regnum ejus sedebit, ut confirmet illud et corroboret in judicio et justitia, amodo et usque in sempiternum : zelus Domini exercituum faciet hoc.]
     EN: His dominion will increase, and there will be no end to peace. He will sit upon David's throne and over his kingdom, to establish it and strengthen it through judgment and righteousness, from now and forever. The zeal of the Lord of hosts will do this.

9:7  LA: [Verbum misit Dominus in Jacob, et cecidit in Israël.
     EN: The Lord sent a word into Jacob, and it fell upon Israel.

9:8  LA: Et sciet omnis populus Ephraim, et habitantes Samariam, in superbia et magnitudine cordis dicentes :
     EN: All the people of Ephraim and the inhabitants of Samaria will know it, who say in pride and arrogance of heart:

9:9  LA: Lateres ceciderunt, sed quadris lapidibus ædificabimus ; sycomoros succiderunt, sed cedros immutabimus.
     EN: Bricks have fallen, but we will build with dressed stones; they have cut down sycamores, but we will replace them with cedars.

9:10  LA: Et elevabit Dominus hostes Rasin super eum, et inimicos ejus in tumultum vertet.
      EN: The Lord will raise Rezin's foes against him and stir his enemies into tumult:

9:11  LA: Syriam ab oriente, et Philisthiim ab occidente ; et devorabunt Israël toto ore. In omnibus his non est aversus furor ejus, sed adhuc manus ejus extenta.
      EN: Syria from the east and the Philistines from the west. They will devour Israel with their whole mouth. Through all this His fury has not turned away, and His hand is still outstretched.

9:12  LA: Et populus non est reversus ad percutientem se, et Dominum exercituum non inquisierunt.
      EN: The people have not returned to the One who strikes them, and they have not sought the Lord of hosts.

9:13  LA: Et disperdet Dominus ab Israël caput et caudam, incurvantem et refrenantem, die una.
      EN: The Lord will destroy from Israel head and tail, the one who bends down and the one who restrains, in a single day.

9:14  LA: Longævus et honorabilis, ipse est caput ; et propheta docens mendacium, ipse est cauda.
      EN: The aged and honored man is the head, and the prophet teaching falsehood is the tail.

9:15  LA: Et erunt qui beatificant populum istum, seducentes ; et qui beatificantur, præcipitati.
      EN: Those who call this people blessed will be deceivers, and those called blessed will be cast headlong.

9:16  LA: Propter hoc super adolescentulis ejus non lætabitur Dominus, et pupillorum ejus et viduarum non miserebitur : quia omnis hypocrita est et nequam, et universum os locutum est stultitiam ; in omnibus his non est aversus furor ejus, sed adhuc manus ejus extenta.
      EN: Therefore the Lord will not rejoice over its young men or have mercy on its orphans and widows, for everyone is a hypocrite and wicked, and every mouth has spoken folly. Through all this His fury has not turned away, and His hand is still outstretched.

9:17  LA: Succensa est enim quasi ignis impietas : veprem et spinam vorabit, et succendetur in densitate saltus, et convolvetur superbia fumi.
      EN: For impiety has been kindled like fire; it will devour brier and thorn, and blaze in the forest's thicket, and roll upward in a towering cloud of smoke.

9:18  LA: In ira Domini exercituum conturbata est terra, et erit populus quasi esca ignis ; vir fratri suo non parcet.
      EN: In the anger of the Lord of hosts the land has been shaken, and the people will be like food for fire; a man will not spare his brother.

9:19  LA: Et declinabit ad dexteram, et esuriet ; et comedet ad sinistram, et non saturabitur ; unusquisque carnem brachii sui vorabit : Manasses Ephraim, et Ephraim Manassen ; simul ipsi contra Judam.
      EN: He will turn to the right and be hungry; he will eat on the left and not be satisfied. Each will devour the flesh of his own arm: Manasseh Ephraim, and Ephraim Manasseh; together they are against Judah.

9:20  LA: In omnibus his non est aversus furor ejus, sed adhuc manus ejus extenta.]
      EN: Through all this His fury has not turned away, and His hand is still outstretched.

ISAIAH 10

10:1  LA: Væ qui condunt leges iniquas, et scribentes injustitiam scripserunt,
      EN: Woe to those who establish unjust laws, and to the writers who have written injustice,

10:2  LA: ut opprimerent in judicio pauperes, et vim facerent causæ humilium populi mei ; ut essent viduæ præda eorum, et pupillos diriperent.
      EN: to oppress the poor in judgment and do violence to the cause of the humble of My people, so that widows become their prey and they plunder orphans.

10:3  LA: Quid facietis in die visitationis, et calamitatis de longe venientis ? ad cujus confugietis auxilium ? et ubi derelinquetis gloriam vestram,
      EN: What will you do on the day of visitation and of calamity coming from afar? To whose help will you flee, and where will you leave your glory,

10:4  LA: ne incurvemini sub vinculo, et cum interfectis cadatis ? Super omnibus his non est aversus furor ejus, sed adhuc manus ejus extenta.]
      EN: so that you do not bow down beneath bonds and fall among the slain? Through all this His fury has not turned away, and His hand is still outstretched.

10:5  LA: [Væ Assur ! virga furoris mei et baculus ipse est ; in manu eorum indignatio mea.
      EN: Woe to Assyria! He is the rod and staff of My fury; My indignation is in their hand.

10:6  LA: Ad gentem fallacem mittam eum, et contra populum furoris mei mandabo illi, ut auferat spolia, et diripiat prædam, et ponat illum in conculcationem quasi lutum platearum.
      EN: I will send him against a deceitful nation and command him against the people of My fury, to carry away spoils and seize plunder and make it something trampled like mud in the streets.

10:7  LA: Ipse autem non sic arbitrabitur, et cor ejus non ita existimabit ; sed ad conterendum erit cor ejus, et ad internecionem gentium non paucarum.
      EN: But he will not think this way, and his heart will not reckon it so; his heart will be set on crushing, on exterminating no few nations.

10:8  LA: Dicet enim :
      EN: For he will say:

10:9  LA: Numquid non principes mei simul reges sunt ? numquid non ut Charcamis, sic Calano ? et ut Arphad, sic Emath ? numquid non ut Damascus, sic Samaria ?
      EN: Are not my rulers all kings? Is not Calno like Carchemish, and Hamath like Arpad? Is not Samaria like Damascus?

10:10  LA: Quomodo invenit manus mea regna idoli, sic et simulacra eorum de Jerusalem et de Samaria.
       EN: As my hand has reached the kingdoms of the idol, so also their images from Jerusalem and Samaria.

10:11  LA: Numquid non sicut feci Samariæ et idolis ejus, sic faciam Jerusalem et simulacris ejus ?
       EN: As I have done to Samaria and its idols, will I not do the same to Jerusalem and its images?

10:12  LA: Et erit, cum impleverit Dominus cuncta opera sua in monte Sion et in Jerusalem, visitabo super fructum magnifici cordis regis Assur, et super gloriam altitudinis oculorum ejus.
       EN: When the Lord has completed all His works on Mount Zion and in Jerusalem, I will punish the fruit of the Assyrian king's proud heart and the glory of his haughty eyes.

10:13  LA: Dixit enim : In fortitudine manus meæ feci, et in sapientia mea intellexi ; et abstuli terminos populorum, et principes eorum deprædatus sum, et detraxi quasi potens in sublimi residentes.
       EN: For he said: I have done it by the strength of my hand, and by my wisdom I have understood. I have removed the boundaries of peoples and plundered their rulers; like a mighty man I have brought down those seated on high.

10:14  LA: Et invenit quasi nidum manus mea fortitudinem populorum ; et sicut colliguntur ova quæ derelicta sunt, sic universam terram ego congregavi ; et non fuit qui moveret pennam, et aperiret os, et ganniret.
       EN: My hand found the strength of peoples as though it were a nest. As abandoned eggs are gathered, so I gathered the whole earth; no one moved a wing or opened a mouth or chirped.

10:15  LA: Numquid gloriabitur securis contra eum qui secat in ea ? aut exaltabitur serra contra eum a quo trahitur ? Quomodo si elevetur virga contra elevantem se, et exaltetur baculus, qui utique lignum est.
       EN: Will the axe boast against the one who cuts with it? Or will the saw exalt itself against the one who draws it? As though a rod were raised against the one lifting it, and a staff exalted itself, though it is only wood!

10:16  LA: Propter hoc mittet Dominator, Dominus exercituum, in pinguibus ejus tenuitatem ; et subtus gloriam ejus succensa ardebit quasi combustio ignis.
       EN: Therefore the Sovereign, the Lord of hosts, will send leanness among his fat ones; beneath his glory a kindled blaze will burn like a consuming fire.

10:17  LA: Et erit lumen Israël in igne, et Sanctus ejus in flamma ; et succendetur, et devorabitur spina ejus et vepres in die una.
       EN: The light of Israel will become a fire and his Holy One a flame. His thorn and briers will be kindled and devoured in a single day.

10:18  LA: Et gloria saltus ejus, et carmeli ejus, ab anima usque ad carnem consumetur ; et erit terrore profugus.
       EN: The glory of his forest and his fruitful field will be consumed, from soul to flesh, and he will flee in terror.

10:19  LA: Et reliquiæ ligni saltus ejus præ paucitate numerabuntur, et puer scribet eos.]
       EN: The remaining trees of his forest will be so few that they can be counted, and a child will write them down.

10:20  LA: [Et erit in die illa : non adjiciet residuum Israël, et hi qui fugerint de domo Jacob, inniti super eo qui percutit eos ; sed innitetur super Dominum, Sanctum Israël, in veritate.
       EN: On that day the remnant of Israel and those of the house of Jacob who have escaped will no longer lean upon the one who strikes them; they will truly lean upon the Lord, the Holy One of Israel.

10:21  LA: Reliquiæ convertentur ; reliquiæ, inquam, Jacob ad Deum fortem.
       EN: A remnant will return—a remnant, I say, of Jacob—to the mighty God.

10:22  LA: Si enim fuerit populus tuus, Israël, quasi arena maris, reliquiæ convertentur ex eo ; consummatio abbreviata inundabit justitiam.
       EN: For though your people, Israel, are like the sand of the sea, a remnant of them will return; a destruction cut short will overflow with righteousness.

10:23  LA: Consummationem enim et abbreviationem Dominus Deus exercituum faciet in medio omnis terræ.
       EN: For the Lord, the God of hosts, will carry out destruction and a cutting short in the midst of the whole land.

10:24  LA: Propter hoc, hæc dicit Dominus Deus exercituum : Noli timere, populus meus, habitator Sion, ab Assur : in virga percutiet te, et baculum suum levabit super te, in via Ægypti.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord, the God of hosts: My people who dwell in Zion, do not fear the Assyrian. He will strike you with a rod and raise his staff against you, as Egypt did.

10:25  LA: Adhuc enim paululum modicumque, et consummabitur indignatio et furor meus super scelus eorum.
       EN: For in a very little while My indignation and fury against their crime will be brought to an end.

10:26  LA: Et suscitabit super eum Dominus exercituum flagellum, juxta plagam Madian in petra Oreb : et virgam suam super mare, et levabit eam in via Ægypti.
       EN: The Lord of hosts will raise a scourge against him, like the blow against Midian at the rock of Oreb; He will raise His rod over the sea, as He did against Egypt.

10:27  LA: Et erit in die illa : auferetur onus ejus de humero tuo et jugum ejus de collo tuo, et computrescet jugum a facie olei.]
       EN: On that day his burden will be removed from your shoulder and his yoke from your neck; the yoke will decay before the oil.

10:28  LA: [Veniet in Ajath, transibit in Magron, apud Machmas commendabit vasa sua.
       EN: He will come to Aiath, pass through Migron, and deposit his equipment at Michmash.

10:29  LA: Transierunt cursim, Gaba sedes nostra ; obstupuit Rama, Gabaath Saulis fugit.
       EN: They have passed through at a run: Geba is our lodging! Ramah has been astonished; Gibeah of Saul has fled.

10:30  LA: Hinni voce tua, filia Gallim, attende Laisa, paupercula Anathoth.
       EN: Cry out with your voice, daughter Gallim! Pay attention, Laishah, poor little Anathoth!

10:31  LA: Migravit Medemena ; habitatores Gabim, confortamini.
       EN: Madmenah has departed; inhabitants of Gebim, take courage!

10:32  LA: Adhuc dies est ut in Nobe stetur ; agitabit manum suam super montem filiæ Sion, collem Jerusalem.
       EN: There is still a day to make a stand at Nob; he will shake his hand over the mountain of daughter Zion, the hill of Jerusalem.

10:33  LA: Ecce Dominator, Dominus exercituum, confringet lagunculam in terrore ; et excelsi statura succidentur, et sublimes humiliabuntur.
       EN: Behold, the Sovereign, the Lord of hosts, will shatter the small jar with terror; the tall in stature will be cut down, and the lofty will be humbled.

10:34  LA: Et subvertentur condensa saltus ferro ; et Libanus cum excelsis cadet.]
       EN: The forest's thickets will be overthrown with iron, and Lebanon will fall with its lofty ones.

ISAIAH 11

11:1  LA: Et egredietur virga de radice Jesse, et flos de radice ejus ascendet.
      EN: A rod will come forth from the root of Jesse, and a flower will rise from his root.

11:2  LA: Et requiescet super eum spiritus Domini : spiritus sapientiæ et intellectus, spiritus consilii et fortitudinis, spiritus scientiæ et pietatis ;
      EN: The spirit of the Lord will rest upon him: the spirit of wisdom and understanding, the spirit of counsel and fortitude, the spirit of knowledge and piety;

11:3  LA: et replebit eum spiritus timoris Domini. Non secundum visionem oculorum judicabit, neque secundum auditum aurium arguet ;
      EN: and the spirit of the fear of the Lord will fill him. He will not judge according to what his eyes see or reprove according to what his ears hear,

11:4  LA: sed judicabit in justitia pauperes, et arguet in æquitate pro mansuetis terræ ; et percutiet terram virga oris sui, et spiritu labiorum suorum interficiet impium.
      EN: but he will judge the poor with righteousness and reprove with equity on behalf of the meek of the earth. He will strike the earth with the rod of his mouth and kill the wicked with the breath of his lips.

11:5  LA: Et erit justitia cingulum lumborum ejus, et fides cinctorium renum ejus.
      EN: Righteousness will be the belt of his waist, and faithfulness the band around his loins.

11:6  LA: Habitabit lupus cum agno, et pardus cum hædo accubabit ; vitulus, et leo, et ovis, simul morabuntur, et puer parvulus minabit eos.
      EN: The wolf will dwell with the lamb, and the leopard will lie down with the kid. Calf and lion and sheep will stay together, and a little child will drive them.

11:7  LA: Vitulus et ursus pascentur, simul requiescent catuli eorum ; et leo quasi bos comedet paleas.
      EN: Calf and bear will graze, their young will rest together, and the lion will eat straw like the ox.

11:8  LA: Et delectabitur infans ab ubere super foramine aspidis ; et in caverna reguli qui ablactatus fuerit manum suam mittet.
      EN: The nursing infant will play over the asp's hole, and the weaned child will put his hand into the basilisk's den.

11:9  LA: Non nocebunt, et non occident in universo monte sancto meo, quia repleta est terra scientia Domini, sicut aquæ maris operientes.]
      EN: They will not harm or kill anywhere on My holy mountain, for the earth is filled with knowledge of the Lord, like the covering waters of the sea.

11:10  LA: [In die illa radix Jesse, qui stat in signum populorum, ipsum gentes deprecabuntur, et erit sepulchrum ejus gloriosum.
       EN: On that day the root of Jesse, who stands as a signal for the peoples—the nations will entreat him, and his tomb will be glorious.

11:11  LA: Et erit in die illa : adjiciet Dominus secundo manum suam ad possidendum residuum populi sui, quod relinquetur ab Assyriis, et ab Ægypto, et a Phetros, et ab Æthiopia, et ab Ælam, et a Sennaar, et ab Emath, et ab insulis maris.
       EN: On that day the Lord will extend His hand a second time to possess the remnant of His people left from the Assyrians, from Egypt, from Pathros, from Ethiopia, from Elam, from Shinar, from Hamath, and from the islands of the sea.

11:12  LA: Et levabit signum in nationes, et congregabit profugos Israël, et dispersos Juda colliget a quatuor plagis terræ.
       EN: He will raise a signal for the nations and gather Israel's fugitives; He will assemble Judah's scattered ones from the four quarters of the earth.

11:13  LA: Et auferetur zelus Ephraim, et hostes Juda peribunt ; Ephraim non æmulabitur Judam, et Judas non pugnabit contra Ephraim.
       EN: Ephraim's jealousy will be removed, and Judah's enemies will perish. Ephraim will not envy Judah, and Judah will not fight against Ephraim.

11:14  LA: Et volabunt in humeros Philisthiim per mare, simul prædabuntur filios orientis ; Idumæa et Moab præceptum manus eorum, et filii Ammon obedientes erunt.
       EN: They will fly upon the shoulders of the Philistines by the sea; together they will plunder the children of the east. Edom and Moab will be under their hand's command, and the children of Ammon will obey.

11:15  LA: Et desolabit Dominus linguam maris Ægypti, et levabit manum suam super flumen in fortitudine spiritus sui ; et percutiet eum in septem rivis, ita ut transeant per eum calceati.
       EN: The Lord will lay waste the tongue of the sea of Egypt and raise His hand over the river in the strength of His spirit. He will strike it into seven streams, so that people may cross it in their sandals.

11:16  LA: Et erit via residuo populo meo qui relinquetur ab Assyriis, sicut fuit Israëli in die illa qua ascendit de terra Ægypti.]
       EN: There will be a road for the remnant of My people left from the Assyrians, as there was for Israel on the day when it came up from the land of Egypt.

ISAIAH 12

12:1  LA: Et dices in die illa : Confitebor tibi, Domine, quoniam iratus es mihi ; conversus est furor tuus, et consolatus es me.
      EN: On that day you will say: I will give thanks to You, Lord, because You were angry with me; Your fury has turned away, and You have comforted me.

12:2  LA: Ecce Deus salvator meus ; fiducialiter agam, et non timebo : quia fortitudo mea et laus mea Dominus, et factus est mihi in salutem.
      EN: Behold, God is my savior. I will act with confidence and not be afraid, for the Lord is my strength and my praise, and He has become my salvation.

12:3  LA: Haurietis aquas in gaudio de fontibus salvatoris.
      EN: You will draw water with joy from the savior's springs.

12:4  LA: Et dicetis in die illa : Confitemini Domino et invocate nomen ejus ; notas facite in populis adinventiones ejus ; mementote quoniam excelsum est nomen ejus.
      EN: On that day you will say: Give thanks to the Lord and invoke His name. Make His deeds known among the peoples; remember that His name is exalted.

12:5  LA: Cantate Domino, quoniam magnifice fecit ; annuntiate hoc in universa terra.
      EN: Sing to the Lord, for He has acted magnificently; announce this throughout the whole earth.

12:6  LA: Exsulta et lauda, habitatio Sion, quia magnus in medio tui Sanctus Israël.]
      EN: Exult and give praise, dwelling of Zion, for great in your midst is the Holy One of Israel.

ISAIAH 13

13:1  LA: Onus Babylonis, quod vidit Isaias, filius Amos.
      EN: The burden concerning Babylon, which Isaiah son of Amoz saw.

13:2  LA: [Super montem caliginosum levate signum : exaltate vocem, levate manum, et ingrediantur portas duces.
      EN: Raise a signal on a dark mountain; lift up your voice, raise your hand, and let the leaders enter the gates.

13:3  LA: Ego mandavi sanctificatis meis, et vocavi fortes meos in ira mea, exsultantes in gloria mea.
      EN: I have given orders to My consecrated ones and summoned My mighty men in My anger, those who exult in My glory.

13:4  LA: Vox multitudinis in montibus, quasi populorum frequentium ; vox sonitus regum, gentium congregatarum. Dominus exercituum præcepit militiæ belli,
      EN: The sound of a multitude on the mountains, like crowded peoples; the sound of the tumult of kings, of nations gathered together. The Lord of hosts has given orders to the army for battle,

13:5  LA: venientibus de terra procul, a summitate cæli ; Dominus, et vasa furoris ejus, ut disperdat omnem terram.
      EN: to those coming from a distant land, from the farthest end of heaven: the Lord and the instruments of His fury, to destroy the whole land.

13:6  LA: Ululate, quia prope est dies Domini ; quasi vastitas a Domino veniet.
      EN: Wail, for the day of the Lord is near; it will come like devastation from the Lord.

13:7  LA: Propter hoc omnes manus dissolventur, et omne cor hominis contabescet,
      EN: Therefore every hand will go slack, and every human heart will melt

13:8  LA: et conteretur. Torsiones et dolores tenebunt ; quasi parturiens dolebunt : unusquisque ad proximum suum stupebit, facies combustæ vultus eorum.
      EN: and be crushed. Torments and pains will seize them; they will suffer like a woman in labor. Each will be astounded at his neighbor; their faces will be faces scorched by fire.

13:9  LA: Ecce dies Domini veniet, crudelis, et indignationis plenus, et iræ, furorisque, ad ponendam terram in solitudinem, et peccatores ejus conterendos de ea.
      EN: Behold, the day of the Lord will come, cruel and full of indignation and anger and fury, to make the land a wilderness and crush its sinners out of it.

13:10  LA: Quoniam stellæ cæli, et splendor earum, non expandent lumen suum ; obtenebratus est sol in ortu suo, et luna non splendebit in lumine suo.
       EN: For the stars of heaven and their brightness will not shed their light; the sun has grown dark at its rising, and the moon will not shine with its light.

13:11  LA: Et visitabo super orbis mala, et contra impios iniquitatem eorum ; et quiescere faciam superbiam infidelium, et arrogantiam fortium humiliabo.
       EN: I will punish the world's evils and the wicked for their iniquity. I will put an end to the pride of the faithless and humble the arrogance of the mighty.

13:12  LA: Pretiosior erit vir auro, et homo mundo obrizo.
       EN: A man will be more precious than gold, and a human being than pure refined gold.

13:13  LA: Super hoc cælum turbabo ; et movebitur terra de loco suo, propter indignationem Domini exercituum, et propter diem iræ furoris ejus.
       EN: Therefore I will shake heaven, and the earth will move from its place, because of the indignation of the Lord of hosts and the day of His furious anger.

13:14  LA: Et erit quasi damula fugiens, et quasi ovis, et non erit qui congreget. Unusquisque ad populum suum convertetur, et singuli ad terram suam fugient.
       EN: It will be like a fleeing doe and like a sheep, with no one to gather it. Everyone will turn to his own people, and each will flee to his own land.

13:15  LA: Omnis qui inventus fuerit occidetur, et omnis qui supervenerit cadet in gladio ;
       EN: Everyone found will be killed, and everyone who comes upon them will fall by the sword.

13:16  LA: infantes eorum allidentur in oculis eorum, diripientur domus eorum, et uxores eorum violabuntur.
       EN: Their infants will be dashed to pieces before their eyes, their houses plundered, and their wives raped.

13:17  LA: Ecce ego suscitabo super eos Medos, qui argentum non quærant, nec aurum velint ;
       EN: Behold, I will stir up against them the Medes, who seek no silver and want no gold;

13:18  LA: sed sagittis parvulos interficient, et lactantibus uteris non miserebuntur, et super filios non parcet oculus eorum.
       EN: instead they will kill little children with arrows. They will have no mercy on mothers who nurse, and their eye will not spare children.

13:19  LA: Et erit Babylon illa gloriosa in regnis, inclyta superbia Chaldæorum, sicut subvertit Dominus Sodomam et Gomorrham.
       EN: Babylon, glorious among kingdoms, the illustrious pride of the Chaldeans, will be as when the Lord overthrew Sodom and Gomorrah.

13:20  LA: Non habitabitur usque in finem, et non fundabitur usque ad generationem et generationem ; nec ponet ibi tentoria Arabs, nec pastores requiescent ibi.
       EN: It will never again be inhabited or founded from generation to generation. No Arab will pitch tents there, and no shepherds will rest there.

13:21  LA: Sed requiescent ibi bestiæ, et replebuntur domus eorum draconibus, et habitabunt ibi struthiones, et pilosi saltabunt ibi ;
       EN: Instead wild beasts will rest there, and their houses will be filled with dragons. Ostriches will dwell there, and shaggy creatures will dance there;

13:22  LA: et respondebunt ibi ululæ in ædibus ejus, et sirenes in delubris voluptatis.]
       EN: owls will answer one another in its houses, and sirens in its shrines of pleasure.

ISAIAH 14

14:1  LA: Prope est ut veniat tempus ejus, et dies ejus non elongabuntur. Miserebitur enim Dominus Jacob, et eliget adhuc de Israël, et requiescere eos faciet super humum suam ; adjungetur advena ad eos, et adhærebit domui Jacob.
      EN: Its time is close at hand, and its days will not be prolonged. For the Lord will have mercy on Jacob and will yet choose from Israel and settle them upon their own soil. The foreigner will join them and cling to the house of Jacob.

14:2  LA: Et tenebunt eos populi, et adducent eos in locum suum ; et possidebit eos domus Israël super terram Domini in servos et ancillas : et erunt capientes eos qui se ceperant, et subjicient exactores suos.]
      EN: Peoples will take them and bring them to their own place, and the house of Israel will possess them in the Lord's land as male and female slaves. They will capture those who had captured them and subject their oppressors.

14:3  LA: [Et erit in die illa : cum requiem dederit tibi Deus a labore tuo, et a concussione tua, et a servitute dura qua ante servisti,
      EN: On that day, when God gives you rest from your toil, your turmoil, and the harsh slavery in which you formerly served,

14:4  LA: sumes parabolam istam contra regem Babylonis, et dices : Quomodo cessavit exactor ; quievit tributum ?
      EN: you will take up this taunt against the king of Babylon and say: How has the oppressor ceased, the tribute come to rest?

14:5  LA: Contrivit Dominus baculum impiorum, virgam dominantium,
      EN: The Lord has broken the staff of the wicked, the rod of rulers,

14:6  LA: cædentem populos in indignatione plaga insanabili, subjicientem in furore gentes, persequentem crudeliter.
      EN: which struck peoples in indignation with an incurable blow, subjected nations in fury, and pursued them cruelly.

14:7  LA: Conquievit et siluit omnis terra, gavisa est et exsultavit ;
      EN: The whole earth has come to rest and fallen silent; it has rejoiced and exulted.

14:8  LA: abietes quoque lætatæ sunt super te, et cedri Libani : ex quo dormisti, non ascendet qui succidat nos.
      EN: Even the fir trees rejoice over you, and the cedars of Lebanon: Since you fell asleep, no one comes up to cut us down.

14:9  LA: Infernus subter conturbatus est in occursum adventus tui ; suscitavit tibi gigantes. Omnes principes terræ surrexerunt de soliis suis, omnes principes nationum.
      EN: The underworld below has been stirred to meet your arrival; it has roused giants for you. All the rulers of the earth have risen from their thrones, all the rulers of the nations.

14:10  LA: Universi respondebunt, et dicent tibi : Et tu vulneratus es sicut et nos ; nostri similis effectus es.
       EN: They will all answer and say to you: You too have been wounded as we have; you have become like us.

14:11  LA: Detracta est ad inferos superbia tua, concidit cadaver tuum ; subter te sternetur tinea, et operimentum tuum erunt vermes.
       EN: Your pride has been brought down to the underworld, and your corpse has fallen. The moth will be spread beneath you, and worms will be your covering.

14:12  LA: Quomodo cecidisti de cælo, Lucifer, qui mane oriebaris ? corruisti in terram, qui vulnerabas gentes ?
       EN: How have you fallen from heaven, morning star, who used to rise at dawn? How you have collapsed upon the earth, you who wounded the nations?

14:13  LA: Qui dicebas in corde tuo : In cælum conscendam, super astra Dei exaltabo solium meum ; sedebo in monte testamenti, in lateribus aquilonis ;
       EN: You said in your heart: I will ascend to heaven; I will exalt my throne above the stars of God. I will sit on the mountain of the covenant, on the northern slopes;

14:14  LA: ascendam super altitudinem nubium, similis ero Altissimo ?
       EN: I will ascend above the heights of the clouds; I will be like the Most High.

14:15  LA: Verumtamen ad infernum detraheris, in profundum laci.
       EN: Yet you will be brought down to the underworld, into the depths of the pit.

14:16  LA: Qui te viderint, ad te inclinabuntur, teque prospicient : Numquid iste est vir qui conturbavit terram, qui concussit regna,
       EN: Those who see you will bend down toward you and look closely at you: Is this the man who shook the earth, who convulsed kingdoms,

14:17  LA: qui posuit orbem desertum, et urbes ejus destruxit, vinctis ejus non aperuit carcerem ?
       EN: who made the world a wilderness and destroyed its cities, who did not open the prison for his captives?

14:18  LA: Omnes reges gentium universi dormierunt in gloria, vir in domo sua ;
       EN: All the kings of the nations have fallen asleep in glory, each in his own house;

14:19  LA: tu autem projectus es de sepulchro tuo, quasi stirps inutilis pollutus, et obvolutus cum his qui interfecti sunt gladio, et descenderunt ad fundamenta laci, quasi cadaver putridum.
       EN: but you have been thrown out of your tomb like a useless shoot, polluted and wrapped up with those killed by the sword who descended to the foundations of the pit, like a rotten corpse.

14:20  LA: Non habebis consortium, neque cum eis in sepultura ; tu enim terram tuam disperdidisti, tu populum tuum occidisti : non vocabitur in æternum semen pessimorum.
       EN: You will have no fellowship with them, even in burial, for you have destroyed your land and killed your people. The offspring of the most wicked will never be named.

14:21  LA: Præparate filios ejus occisioni, in iniquitate patrum suorum : non consurgent, nec hæreditabunt terram, neque implebunt faciem orbis civitatum.
       EN: Prepare his children for slaughter because of their fathers' iniquity. They will not rise or inherit the land or fill the face of the world with cities.

14:22  LA: Et consurgam super eos, dicit Dominus exercituum ; et perdam Babylonis nomen, et reliquias, et germen, et progeniem, dicit Dominus ;
       EN: I will rise against them, says the Lord of hosts, and destroy Babylon's name and remnant, its shoot and descendants, says the Lord.

14:23  LA: et ponam eam in possessionem ericii, et in paludes aquarum, et scopabo eam in scopa terens, dicit Dominus exercituum.]
       EN: I will make it a possession for the hedgehog and pools of water; I will sweep it away with a broom, wearing it down, says the Lord of hosts.

14:24  LA: [Juravit Dominus exercituum, dicens : Si non, ut putavi, ita erit ; et quomodo mente tractavi,
