       EN: Because of the iniquity of his greed I was angry and struck him. I hid My face from you and was indignant, and he went wandering in the way of his heart.

57:18  LA: Vias ejus vidi, et sanavi eum ; et reduxi eum, et reddidi consolationes ipsi, et lugentibus ejus.
       EN: I saw his ways and healed him; I led him back and restored comfort to him and to those who mourn for him.

57:19  LA: Creavi fructum labiorum pacem ; pacem ei qui longe est et qui prope, dixit Dominus, et sanavi eum.
       EN: I created the fruit of the lips: peace, peace to the one far away and to the one near, says the Lord, and I healed him.

57:20  LA: Impii autem quasi mare fervens, quod quiescere non potest, et redundant fluctus ejus in conculcationem et lutum.
       EN: But the wicked are like a boiling sea that cannot rest, whose waves overflow with churned-up mire.

57:21  LA: Non est pax impiis, dicit Dominus Deus.]
       EN: There is no peace for the wicked, says the Lord God.

ISAIAH 58

58:1  LA: Clama , ne cesses, quasi tuba exalta vocem tuam, et annuntia populo meo scelera eorum, et domui Jacob peccata eorum.
      EN: Cry out without ceasing; lift your voice like a trumpet. Announce to My people their crimes and to the house of Jacob their sins.

58:2  LA: Me etenim de die in diem quærunt, et scire vias meas volunt, quasi gens quæ justitiam fecerit, et judicium Dei sui non dereliquerit. Rogant me judicia justitiæ ; appropinquare Deo volunt.
      EN: For day after day they seek Me and desire to know My ways, like a nation that has practiced justice and not forsaken its God's judgment. They ask Me for just judgments; they wish to draw near to God.

58:3  LA: Quare jejunavimus, et non aspexisti ; humiliavimus animas nostras, et nescisti ? Ecce in die jejunii vestri invenitur voluntas vestra, et omnes debitores vestros repetitis.
      EN: “Why have we fasted and You have not looked, humbled our souls and You have not noticed?” Look, on the day of your fast you pursue your own desire and press all your debtors for payment.

58:4  LA: Ecce ad lites et contentiones jejunatis, et percutitis pugno impie. Nolite jejunare sicut usque ad hanc diem, ut audiatur in excelso clamor vester.
      EN: Look, you fast for quarrels and disputes and strike wickedly with the fist. Do not fast as you have until this day, so that your cry may be heard on high.

58:5  LA: Numquid tale est jejunium quod elegi, per diem affligere hominem animam suam ? numquid contorquere quasi circulum caput suum, et saccum et cinerem sternere ? numquid istud vocabis jejunium, et diem acceptabilem Domino ?]
      EN: Is this the fast I have chosen, for a person to afflict his soul for a day? To bend his head like a circle and spread sackcloth and ashes? Will you call this a fast and a day acceptable to the Lord?

58:6  LA: [Nonne hoc est magis jejunium quod elegi ? Dissolve colligationes impietatis, solve fasciculos deprimentes, dimitte eos qui confracti sunt liberos, et omne onus dirumpe ;
      EN: Is not this rather the fast I have chosen? Loosen the bonds of wickedness, undo the bundles that weigh people down, let the broken go free, and tear apart every burden.

58:7  LA: frange esurienti panem tuum, et egenos vagosque induc in domum tuam ; cum videris nudum, operi eum, et carnem tuam ne despexeris.
      EN: Break your bread for the hungry and bring the needy and homeless into your house. When you see someone naked, cover him, and do not despise your own flesh.

58:8  LA: Tunc erumpet quasi mane lumen tuum ; et sanitas tua citius orietur, et anteibit faciem tuam justitia tua, et gloria Domini colliget te.
      EN: Then your light will break forth like morning, and your healing will spring up swiftly. Your justice will go before your face, and the glory of the Lord will gather you.

58:9  LA: Tunc invocabis, et Dominus exaudiet ; clamabis, et dicet : Ecce adsum. Si abstuleris de medio tui catenam, et desieris extendere digitum et loqui quod non prodest ;
      EN: Then you will call, and the Lord will hear; you will cry out, and He will say, “Look, I am here.” If you remove the chain from your midst and stop pointing the finger and speaking what does no good,

58:10  LA: cum effuderis esurienti animam tuam, et animam afflictam repleveris, orietur in tenebris lux tua, et tenebræ tuæ erunt sicut meridies.
       EN: if you pour out your soul for the hungry and satisfy the afflicted soul, your light will rise in darkness, and your darkness will be like noon.

58:11  LA: Et requiem tibi dabit Dominus semper, et implebit splendoribus animam tuam, et ossa tua liberabit ; et eris quasi hortus irriguus, et sicut fons aquarum cujus non deficient aquæ.
       EN: The Lord will always give you rest, fill your soul with brightness, and deliver your bones. You will be like a watered garden, like a spring of water whose waters never fail.

58:12  LA: Et ædificabuntur in te deserta sæculorum, fundamenta generationis et generationis suscitabis ; et vocaberis ædificator sepium, avertens semitas in quietem.
       EN: In you the ancient ruins will be rebuilt; you will raise up foundations from generation after generation. You will be called the Builder of Enclosures, the One Who Turns Paths toward Rest.

58:13  LA: Si averteris a sabbato pedem tuum facere voluntatem tuam in die sancto meo, et vocaveris sabbatum delicatum, et sanctum Domini gloriosum, et glorificaveris eum dum non facis vias tuas, et non invenitur voluntas tua, ut loquaris sermonem :
       EN: If you turn your foot away from doing your own will on the Sabbath, on My holy day, and call the Sabbath a delight and the Lord's holy day glorious; if you honor it by not following your own ways or pursuing your own desire to speak your own words,

58:14  LA: tunc delectaberis super Domino, et sustollam te super altitudines terræ, et cibabo te hæreditate Jacob patris tui : os enim Domini locutum est.]
       EN: then you will delight in the Lord. I will lift you upon the heights of the earth and feed you with the inheritance of Jacob your father, for the mouth of the Lord has spoken.

ISAIAH 59

59:1  LA: Ecce non est abbreviata manus Domini, ut salvare nequeat, neque aggravata est auris ejus, ut non exaudiat.
      EN: Look, the Lord's hand has not been shortened so that it cannot save, nor has His ear grown heavy so that it cannot hear.

59:2  LA: Sed iniquitates vestræ diviserunt inter vos et Deum vestrum ; et peccata vestra absconderunt faciem ejus a vobis, ne exaudiret.
      EN: But your iniquities have made a division between you and your God, and your sins have hidden His face from you, so that He does not hear.

59:3  LA: Manus enim vestræ pollutæ sunt sanguine, et digiti vestri iniquitate ; labia vestra locuta sunt mendacium, et lingua vestra iniquitatem fatur.
      EN: For your hands are polluted with blood and your fingers with iniquity. Your lips have spoken lies, and your tongue utters wickedness.

59:4  LA: Non est qui invocet justitiam, neque est qui judicet vere : sed confidunt in nihilo, et loquuntur vanitates ; conceperunt laborem, et pepererunt iniquitatem.
      EN: No one calls for justice, and no one judges truly. They trust in nothingness and speak empty things; they conceive misery and bring forth iniquity.

59:5  LA: Ova aspidum ruperunt, et telas araneæ texuerunt. Qui comederit de ovis eorum, morietur ; et quod confotum est, erumpet in regulum.
      EN: They have broken open asps' eggs and woven spiders' webs. Whoever eats their eggs will die, and what is hatched will burst forth as a basilisk.

59:6  LA: Telæ eorum non erunt in vestimentum, neque operientur operibus suis ; opera eorum opera inutilia, et opus iniquitatis in manibus eorum.
      EN: Their webs will not become clothing, nor will they cover themselves with their works. Their works are useless works, and a work of iniquity is in their hands.

59:7  LA: Pedes eorum ad malum currunt, et festinant ut effundant sanguinem innocentem ; cogitationes eorum cogitationes inutiles : vastitas et contritio in viis eorum.
      EN: Their feet run to evil and hurry to shed innocent blood. Their thoughts are worthless thoughts; devastation and destruction are in their ways.

59:8  LA: Viam pacis nescierunt, et non est judicium in gressibus eorum ; semitæ eorum incurvatæ sunt eis : omnis qui calcat in eis, ignorat pacem.
      EN: They have not known the way of peace, and there is no judgment in their steps. Their paths have become crooked for them; everyone who treads them is ignorant of peace.

59:9  LA: Propter hoc elongatum est judicium a nobis, et non apprehendet nos justitia. Exspectavimus lucem, et ecce tenebræ ; splendorem, et in tenebris ambulavimus.
      EN: Therefore judgment is far from us, and justice will not overtake us. We waited for light, and look, darkness; for brightness, and we walked in darkness.

59:10  LA: Palpavimus sicut cæci parietem, et quasi absque oculis attrectavimus : impegimus meridie quasi in tenebris ; in caliginosis quasi mortui.
       EN: We have felt along the wall like the blind and groped as though without eyes. We have stumbled at noon as in darkness; in gloomy places we are like the dead.

59:11  LA: Rugiemus quasi ursi omnes, et quasi columbæ meditantes gememus : exspectavimus judicium, et non est ; salutem, et elongata est a nobis.
       EN: We will all growl like bears and moan like doves murmuring. We waited for judgment, and there is none; for salvation, and it is far from us.

59:12  LA: Multiplicatæ sunt enim iniquitates nostræ coram te, et peccata nostra responderunt nobis, quia scelera nostra nobiscum et iniquitates nostras cognovimus.
       EN: For our iniquities have multiplied before You, and our sins have testified against us. Our crimes are with us, and we have known our iniquities:

59:13  LA: Peccare et mentiri contra Dominum, et aversi sumus ne iremus post tergum Dei nostri, ut loqueremur calumniam et transgressionem ; concepimus et locuti sumus de corde verba mendacii.
       EN: sinning and lying against the Lord. We have turned away from following our God to speak oppression and transgression; we have conceived and uttered lying words from the heart.

59:14  LA: Et conversum est retrorsum judicium, et justitia longe stetit, quia corruit in platea veritas, et æquitas non potuit ingredi.
       EN: Judgment has been turned backward, and justice has stood far away, for truth has fallen in the square, and uprightness could not enter.

59:15  LA: Et facta est veritas in oblivionem, et qui recessit a malo, prædæ patuit. Et vidit Dominus, et malum apparuit in oculis ejus, quia non est judicium.
       EN: Truth has been forgotten, and the one who turns away from evil is exposed to plunder. The Lord saw, and it was evil in His eyes that there was no judgment.

59:16  LA: Et vidit quia non est vir, et aporiatus est, quia non est qui occurrat ; et salvavit sibi brachium suum, et justitia ejus ipsa confirmavit eum.
       EN: He saw that there was no man and was astonished that no one intervened. His own arm brought Him salvation, and His justice itself upheld Him.

59:17  LA: Indutus est justitia ut lorica, et galea salutis in capite ejus ; indutus est vestimentis ultionis, et opertus est quasi pallio zeli :
       EN: He clothed Himself with justice as a breastplate and put the helmet of salvation upon His head. He clothed Himself with garments of vengeance and wrapped Himself in zeal as in a cloak,

59:18  LA: sicut ad vindictam quasi ad retributionem indignationis hostibus suis, et vicissitudinem inimicis suis ; insulis vicem reddet.
       EN: for vengeance, as repayment of indignation to His foes and recompense to His enemies. He will repay the islands in kind.

59:19  LA: Et timebunt qui ab occidente nomen Domini, et qui ab ortu solis gloriam ejus, cum venerit quasi fluvius violentus quem spiritus Domini cogit ;
       EN: Those from the west will fear the Lord's name, and those from the rising sun His glory, when He comes like a violent river driven by the Spirit of the Lord,

59:20  LA: et venerit Sion redemptor, et eis qui redeunt ab iniquitate in Jacob, dicit Dominus.
       EN: and a Redeemer comes to Zion and to those in Jacob who turn back from iniquity, says the Lord.

59:21  LA: Hoc fœdus meum cum eis, dicit Dominus : spiritus meus qui est in te, et verba mea quæ posui in ore tuo, non recedent de ore tuo, et de ore seminis tui, et de ore seminis seminis tui, dicit Dominus, amodo et usque in sempiternum.]
       EN: This is My covenant with them, says the Lord: My Spirit who is in you, and My words that I have placed in your mouth, will not depart from your mouth, from the mouth of your offspring, or from the mouth of your offspring's offspring, says the Lord, from now and forever.

ISAIAH 60

60:1  LA: Surge , illuminare, Jerusalem, quia venit lumen tuum, et gloria Domini super te orta est.
      EN: Arise, be illumined, Jerusalem, for your light has come, and the Lord's glory has risen upon you.

60:2  LA: Quia ecce tenebræ operient terram, et caligo populos ; super te autem orietur Dominus, et gloria ejus in te videbitur.
      EN: For look, darkness will cover the earth and gloom the peoples, but the Lord will rise upon you, and His glory will be seen in you.

60:3  LA: Et ambulabunt gentes in lumine tuo, et reges in splendore ortus tui.
      EN: Nations will walk in your light and kings in the brightness of your rising.

60:4  LA: Leva in circuitu oculos tuos, et vide : omnes isti congregati sunt, venerunt tibi ; filii tui de longe venient et filiæ tuæ de latere surgent.
      EN: Lift your eyes all around and see: all these have gathered and come to you. Your sons will come from afar, and your daughters will rise at your side.

60:5  LA: Tunc videbis, et afflues ; mirabitur et dilatabitur cor tuum : quando conversa fuerit ad te multitudo maris ; fortitudo gentium venerit tibi.
      EN: Then you will see and overflow; your heart will marvel and expand, when the multitude of the sea turns to you and the strength of nations comes to you.

60:6  LA: Inundatio camelorum operiet te, dromedarii Madian et Epha ; omnes de Saba venient, aurum et thus deferentes, et laudem Domino annuntiantes.
      EN: A flood of camels will cover you, the dromedaries of Midian and Ephah. All will come from Sheba, bringing gold and incense and announcing praise to the Lord.

60:7  LA: Omne pecus Cedar congregabitur tibi ; arietes Nabaioth ministrabunt tibi : offerentur super placabili altari meo, et domum majestatis meæ glorificabo.
      EN: All Kedar's flocks will be gathered to you; Nebaioth's rams will serve you. They will be offered upon My altar of favor, and I will glorify the house of My majesty.

60:8  LA: Qui sunt isti qui ut nubes volant, et quasi columbæ ad fenestras suas ?
      EN: Who are these who fly like clouds, like doves to their windows?

60:9  LA: Me enim insulæ exspectant, et naves maris in principio, ut adducam filios tuos de longe ; argentum eorum, et aurum eorum cum eis, nomini Domini Dei tui, et Sancto Israël, quia glorificavit te.
      EN: For the islands await Me, and the ships of the sea at the forefront, so that I may bring your children from far away, their silver and gold with them, for the name of the Lord your God and for the Holy One of Israel, because He has glorified you.

60:10  LA: Et ædificabunt filii peregrinorum muros tuos, et reges eorum ministrabunt tibi ; in indignatione enim mea percussi te, et in reconciliatione mea misertus sum tui.
       EN: Foreigners' sons will build your walls, and their kings will serve you. For in My indignation I struck you, and in My reconciliation I had mercy on you.

60:11  LA: Et aperientur portæ tuæ jugiter ; die ac nocte non claudentur, ut afferatur ad te fortitudo gentium, et reges earum adducantur.
       EN: Your gates will be continually open, not shut by day or night, so that the strength of nations may be brought to you and their kings led in.

60:12  LA: Gens enim et regnum quod non servierit tibi peribit, et gentes solitudine vastabuntur.
       EN: For the nation and kingdom that will not serve you will perish, and nations will be laid waste in desolation.

60:13  LA: Gloria Libani ad te veniet, abies, et buxus, et pinus simul ad ornandum locum sanctificationis meæ ; et locum pedum meorum glorificabo.
       EN: Lebanon's glory will come to you, fir, box tree, and pine together, to adorn the place of My sanctuary; I will glorify the place of My feet.

60:14  LA: Et venient ad te curvi filii eorum qui humiliaverunt te, et adorabunt vestigia pedum tuorum omnes qui detrahebant tibi : et vocabunt te civitatem Domini, Sion Sancti Israël.
       EN: The sons of those who humiliated you will come bowing to you, and all who disparaged you will bow at your feet. They will call you the City of the Lord, Zion of the Holy One of Israel.

60:15  LA: Pro eo quod fuisti derelicta et odio habita, et non erat qui per te transiret : ponam te in superbiam sæculorum, gaudium in generationem et generationem :
       EN: Because you were forsaken and hated, with no one passing through you, I will make you an everlasting pride, a joy from generation to generation.

60:16  LA: et suges lac gentium, et mamilla regum lactaberis ; et scies quia ego Dominus salvans te, et redemptor tuus, Fortis Jacob.
       EN: You will suck the milk of nations and be nursed at the breast of kings. You will know that I am the Lord who saves you, your Redeemer, the Mighty One of Jacob.

60:17  LA: Pro ære afferam aurum, et pro ferro afferam argentum, et pro lignis æs, et pro lapidibus ferrum : et ponam visitationem tuam pacem, et præpositos tuos justitiam.
       EN: In place of bronze I will bring gold, and in place of iron silver; in place of wood bronze, and in place of stones iron. I will make peace your overseer and justice your rulers.

60:18  LA: Non audietur ultra iniquitas in terra tua ; vastitas et contritio in terminis tuis : et occupabit salus muros tuos, et portas tuas laudatio.
       EN: Iniquity will no longer be heard in your land, nor devastation and destruction within your borders. Salvation will occupy your walls and praise your gates.

60:19  LA: Non erit tibi amplius sol ad lucendum per diem, nec splendor lunæ illuminabit te : sed erit tibi Dominus in lucem sempiternam, et Deus tuus in gloriam tuam.
       EN: The sun will no longer be your light by day, nor will the moon's brightness illuminate you. The Lord will be your everlasting light, and your God your glory.

60:20  LA: Non occidet ultra sol tuus, et luna tua non minuetur, quia erit tibi Dominus in lucem sempiternam, et complebuntur dies luctus tui.
       EN: Your sun will no longer set, and your moon will not wane, for the Lord will be your everlasting light, and your days of mourning will be ended.

60:21  LA: Populus autem tuus omnes justi ; in perpetuum hæreditabunt terram : germen plantationis meæ, opus manus meæ ad glorificandum.
       EN: Your people will all be just; they will inherit the land forever, a shoot of My planting, the work of My hand for My glory.

60:22  LA: Minimus erit in mille, et parvulus in gentem fortissimam. Ego Dominus in tempore ejus subito faciam istud.]
       EN: The least will become a thousand, and the little one a mighty nation. I, the Lord, will do this suddenly in its time.

ISAIAH 61

61:1  LA: Spiritus Domini super me, eo quod unxerit Dominus me ; ad annuntiandum mansuetis misit me, ut mederer contritis corde, et prædicarem captivis indulgentiam, et clausis apertionem ;
      EN: The Spirit of the Lord is upon me, because the Lord has anointed me. He sent me to announce good news to the meek, to heal the brokenhearted, to proclaim release to captives and opening to those confined,

61:2  LA: ut prædicarem annum placabilem Domino, et diem ultionis Deo nostro ; ut consolarer omnes lugentes,
      EN: to proclaim a year acceptable to the Lord and a day of vengeance for our God, to comfort all who mourn,

61:3  LA: ut ponerem lugentibus Sion, et darem eis coronam pro cinere, oleum gaudii pro luctu, pallium laudis pro spiritu mœroris ; et vocabuntur in ea fortes justitiæ, plantatio Domini ad glorificandum.
      EN: to provide for those mourning in Zion and give them a crown in place of ashes, the oil of joy in place of mourning, a mantle of praise in place of a grieving spirit. In her they will be called the mighty ones of justice, the Lord's planting for His glory.

61:4  LA: Et ædificabunt deserta a sæculo, et ruinas antiquas erigent, et instaurabunt civitates desertas, dissipatas in generationem et generationem.
      EN: They will build up places long deserted and raise up ancient ruins. They will restore deserted cities, devastated from generation to generation.

61:5  LA: Et stabunt alieni, et pascent pecora vestra, et filii peregrinorum agricolæ et vinitores vestri erunt.
      EN: Strangers will stand and feed your flocks, and foreigners' sons will be your farmers and vinedressers.

61:6  LA: Vos autem sacerdotes Domini vocabimini : Ministri Dei nostri, dicetur vobis, fortitudinem gentium comedetis, et in gloria earum superbietis.
      EN: But you will be called priests of the Lord. They will call you ministers of our God. You will feed on the strength of nations and boast in their glory.

61:7  LA: Pro confusione vestra duplici et rubore, laudabunt partem suam ; propter hoc in terra sua duplicia possidebunt, lætitia sempiterna erit eis.
      EN: In place of your double shame and disgrace, they will praise their portion. Therefore they will possess a double share in their land, and everlasting gladness will be theirs.

61:8  LA: Quia ego Dominus diligens judicium, et odio habens rapinam in holocausto ; et dabo opus eorum in veritate, et fœdus perpetuum feriam eis.
      EN: For I am the Lord who loves judgment and hates robbery in a burnt offering. I will repay their work faithfully and make an everlasting covenant with them.

61:9  LA: Et scient in gentibus semen eorum, et germen eorum in medio populorum ; omnes qui viderint eos cognoscent illos, quia isti sunt semen cui benedixit Dominus.
      EN: Their offspring will be known among the nations and their descendants among the peoples. All who see them will recognize that they are the offspring whom the Lord has blessed.

61:10  LA: Gaudens gaudebo in Domino, et exsultabit anima mea in Deo meo, quia induit me vestimentis salutis, et indumento justitiæ circumdedit me, quasi sponsum decoratum corona, et quasi sponsam ornatam monilibus suis.
       EN: I will greatly rejoice in the Lord, and my soul will exult in my God, for He has clothed me with garments of salvation and wrapped me in a robe of justice, like a bridegroom adorned with a crown and a bride adorned with her jewels.

61:11  LA: Sicut enim terra profert germen suum, et sicut hortus semen suum germinat, sic Dominus Deus germinabit justitiam et laudem coram universis gentibus.]
       EN: For as the earth brings forth its growth and a garden makes its seed sprout, so the Lord God will make justice and praise spring up before all nations.

ISAIAH 62

62:1  LA: Propter Sion non tacebo, et propter Jerusalem non quiescam, donec egrediatur ut splendor justus ejus, et salvator ejus ut lampas accendatur.
      EN: For Zion's sake I will not be silent, and for Jerusalem's sake I will not rest, until her just one comes forth like brightness and her savior burns like a torch.

62:2  LA: Et videbunt gentes justum tuum, et cuncti reges inclytum tuum ; et vocabitur tibi nomen novum, quod os Domini nominabit.
      EN: Nations will see your just one, and all kings your glorious one. You will be called by a new name that the mouth of the Lord will name.

62:3  LA: Et eris corona gloriæ in manu Domini, et diadema regni in manu Dei tui.
      EN: You will be a crown of glory in the Lord's hand and a royal diadem in the hand of your God.

62:4  LA: Non vocaberis ultra Derelicta, et terra tua non vocabitur amplius Desolata ; sed vocaberis, Voluntas mea in ea, et terra tua Inhabitata, quia complacuit Domino in te, et terra tua inhabitabitur.
      EN: You will no longer be called Forsaken, nor will your land be called Desolate. You will be called My Delight Is in Her, and your land Inhabited, for the Lord has delighted in you, and your land will be inhabited.

62:5  LA: Habitabit enim juvenis cum virgine, et habitabunt in te filii tui ; et gaudebit sponsus super sponsam, et gaudebit super te Deus tuus.
      EN: For a young man will dwell with a virgin, and your children will dwell in you. A bridegroom will rejoice over his bride, and your God will rejoice over you.

62:6  LA: Super muros tuos, Jerusalem, constitui custodes ; tota die et tota nocte in perpetuum non tacebunt. Qui reminiscimini Domini, ne taceatis,
      EN: Upon your walls, Jerusalem, I have appointed watchmen; all day and all night they will never be silent. You who remember the Lord, do not be silent,

62:7  LA: et ne detis silentium ei, donec stabiliat et donec ponat Jerusalem laudem in terra.
      EN: and give Him no silence until He establishes Jerusalem and makes her a praise in the earth.

62:8  LA: Juravit Dominus in dextera sua, et in brachio fortitudinis suæ : Si dedero triticum tuum ultra cibum inimicis tuis ; et si biberint filii alieni vinum tuum in quo laborasti.
      EN: The Lord has sworn by His right hand and His mighty arm: I will never again give your wheat as food for your enemies, nor will foreigners drink the wine for which you have labored.

62:9  LA: Quia qui congregant illud, comedent, et laudabunt Dominum ; et qui comportant illud, bibent in atriis sanctis meis.
      EN: Those who gather it will eat it and praise the Lord; those who bring it in will drink it in My holy courts.

62:10  LA: Transite, transite per portas, præparate viam populo : planum facite iter, eligite lapides, et elevate signum ad populos.
       EN: Pass through, pass through the gates; prepare the road for the people. Make the way level, remove the stones, and raise a standard for the peoples.

62:11  LA: Ecce Dominus auditum fecit in extremis terræ : Dicite filiæ Sion : Ecce Salvator tuus venit ; ecce merces ejus cum eo, et opus ejus coram illo.
       EN: Look, the Lord has made it heard to the ends of the earth: Say to daughter Zion, “Look, your Savior comes. Look, His reward is with Him, and His work before Him.”

62:12  LA: Et vocabunt eos, Populus sanctus, redempti a Domino ; tu autem vocaberis, Quæsita civitas, et non Derelicta.]
       EN: They will call them the Holy People, the Redeemed of the Lord. And you will be called the Sought-Out City, and not Forsaken.

ISAIAH 63

63:1  LA: Quis est iste, qui venit de Edom, tinctis vestibus de Bosra ? iste formosus in stola sua, gradiens in multitudine fortitudinis suæ ? Ego qui loquor justitiam, et propugnator sum ad salvandum.
      EN: Who is this coming from Edom, with dyed garments from Bozrah, splendid in his robe, advancing in the abundance of his strength? “I, who speak justice and am a champion to save.”

63:2  LA: Quare ergo rubrum est indumentum tuum, et vestimenta tua sicut calcantium in torculari ?
      EN: Why, then, is Your clothing red, and Your garments like those of people treading a winepress?

63:3  LA: Torcular calcavi solus, et de gentibus non est vir mecum ; calcavi eos in furore meo, et conculcavi eos in ira mea : et aspersus est sanguis eorum super vestimenta mea, et omnia indumenta mea inquinavi.
      EN: I have trodden the winepress alone, and no man from the nations was with Me. I trod them in My fury and trampled them in My anger. Their blood spattered My garments, and I stained all My clothing.

63:4  LA: Dies enim ultionis in corde meo ; annus redemptionis meæ venit.
      EN: For a day of vengeance is in My heart; the year of My redemption has come.

63:5  LA: Circumspexi, et non erat auxiliator ; quæsivi, et non fuit qui adjuvaret : et salvavit mihi brachium meum, et indignatio mea ipsa auxiliata est mihi.
      EN: I looked around, and there was no helper; I searched, and there was no one to help. My own arm brought Me salvation, and My indignation itself helped Me.

63:6  LA: Et conculcavi populos in furore meo, et inebriavi eos in indignatione mea, et detraxi in terram virtutem eorum.]
      EN: I trampled peoples in My fury, made them drunk in My indignation, and brought their strength down to the earth.

63:7  LA: [Miserationum Domini recordabor ; laudem Domini super omnibus quæ reddidit nobis Dominus, et super multitudinem bonorum domui Israël, quæ largitus est eis secundum indulgentiam suam, et secundum multitudinem misericordiarum suarum.
      EN: I will remember the Lord's mercies, the Lord's praise for all the Lord has bestowed on us and for the multitude of good things for the house of Israel, which He lavished upon them according to His kindness and His abundant mercies.

63:8  LA: Et dixit : Verumtamen populus meus est, filii non negantes ; et factus est eis salvator.
      EN: He said, “Surely they are My people, children who will not deny Me.” And He became their Savior.

63:9  LA: In omni tribulatione eorum non est tribulatus, et angelus faciei ejus salvavit eos : in dilectione sua et in indulgentia sua ipse redemit eos, et portavit eos, et elevavit eos cunctis diebus sæculi.
      EN: In all their distress He was not distressed, and the angel of His presence saved them. In His love and His kindness He Himself redeemed them, carried them, and lifted them up all the days of old.

63:10  LA: Ipsi autem ad iracundiam provocaverunt, et afflixerunt spiritum Sancti ejus : et conversus est eis in inimicum, et ipse debellavit eos.
       EN: But they provoked Him to anger and grieved the Spirit of His Holy One. He turned into their enemy and Himself fought against them.

63:11  LA: Et recordatus est dierum sæculi Moysi, et populi sui. Ubi est qui eduxit eos de mari cum pastoribus gregis sui ? Ubi est qui posuit in medio ejus spiritum Sancti sui ;
       EN: He remembered the ancient days of Moses and His people. Where is He who brought them up from the sea with the shepherds of His flock? Where is He who placed the Spirit of His Holy One in their midst,

63:12  LA: qui eduxit ad dexteram Moysen, brachio majestatis suæ ; qui scidit aquas ante eos, ut faceret sibi nomen sempiternum ;
       EN: who led Moses at the right hand by His majestic arm, who divided the waters before them to make Himself an everlasting name,

63:13  LA: qui eduxit eos per abyssos, quasi equum in deserto non impingentem ?
       EN: who led them through the depths like a horse in the desert that does not stumble?

63:14  LA: Quasi animal in campo descendens, spiritus Domini ductor ejus fuit. Sic adduxisti populum tuum, ut faceres tibi nomen gloriæ.
       EN: Like an animal descending into a plain, the Spirit of the Lord was its guide. So You led Your people to make Yourself a glorious name.

63:15  LA: Attende de cælo, et vide de habitaculo sancto tuo, et gloriæ tuæ. Ubi est zelus tuus, et fortitudo tua, multitudo viscerum tuorum et miserationum tuarum ? Super me continuerunt se.
       EN: Look down from heaven and see from Your holy and glorious dwelling. Where are Your zeal and Your strength, Your abundant tender feelings and mercies? They have held themselves back from me.

63:16  LA: Tu enim pater noster : et Abraham nescivit nos, et Israël ignoravit nos : tu, Domine, pater noster, redemptor noster, a sæculo nomen tuum.
       EN: For You are our Father. Abraham has not known us, and Israel has not recognized us. You, Lord, are our Father, our Redeemer; from of old is Your name.

63:17  LA: Quare errare nos fecisti, Domine, de viis tuis ; indurasti cor nostrum ne timeremus te ? Convertere propter servos tuos, tribus hæreditatis tuæ.
       EN: Why have You made us stray from Your ways, Lord, and hardened our heart so that we do not fear You? Return for the sake of Your servants, the tribes of Your inheritance.

63:18  LA: Quasi nihilum possederunt populum sanctum tuum : hostes nostri conculcaverunt sanctificationem tuam.
       EN: They possessed Your holy people as though they were nothing; our enemies trampled Your sanctuary.

63:19  LA: Facti sumus quasi in principio, cum non dominareris nostri, neque invocaretur nomen tuum super nos.]
       EN: We have become as at the beginning, when You did not rule over us and Your name was not called upon us.

ISAIAH 64

64:1  LA: sicut exustio ignis tabescerent, aquæ arderent igni : ut notum fieret nomen tuum inimicis tuis ; a facie tua gentes turbarentur.
      EN: melt as at fire's burning, and waters would boil in the fire, so that Your name might become known to Your enemies and nations tremble before You!

64:2  LA: Cum feceris mirabilia, non sustinebimus ; descendisti, et a facie tua montes defluxerunt.
      EN: When You do marvelous things, we will not withstand them. You came down, and the mountains melted before Your face.

64:3  LA: A sæculo non audierunt, neque auribus perceperunt ; oculus non vidit, Deus, absque te, quæ præparasti exspectantibus te.
      EN: From of old they have not heard or perceived with their ears; no eye has seen, O God, apart from You, what You have prepared for those who wait for You.

64:4  LA: Occurristi lætanti, et facienti justitiam ; in viis tuis recordabuntur tui. Ecce tu iratus es, et peccavimus ; in ipsis fuimus semper, et salvabimur.
      EN: You met the one who rejoices and practices justice; in Your ways they will remember You. Look, You are angry, and we have sinned. We have always been in these ways, and we will be saved.

64:5  LA: Et facti sumus ut immundus omnes nos, et quasi pannus menstruatæ universæ justitiæ nostræ ; et cecidimus quasi folium universi, et iniquitates nostræ quasi ventus abstulerunt nos.
      EN: We have all become like an unclean person, and all our righteous deeds like a menstruating woman's cloth. We have all fallen like a leaf, and our iniquities have carried us away like the wind.

64:6  LA: Non est qui invocet nomen tuum ; qui consurgat, et teneat te. Abscondisti faciem tuam a nobis, et allisisti nos in manu iniquitatis nostræ.
      EN: There is no one who calls upon Your name, who rises to take hold of You. You have hidden Your face from us and crushed us in the hand of our iniquity.

64:7  LA: Et nunc, Domine, pater noster es tu, nos vero lutum ; et fictor noster tu, et opera manuum tuarum omnes nos.
      EN: But now, Lord, You are our Father, and we are clay. You are our Maker, and we are all the work of Your hands.

64:8  LA: Ne irascaris, Domine, satis, et ne ultra memineris iniquitatis nostræ ; ecce, respice, populus tuus omnes nos.
      EN: Do not be exceedingly angry, Lord, or remember our iniquity any longer. Look, consider: we are all Your people.

64:9  LA: Civitas Sancti tui facta est deserta, Sion deserta facta est, Jerusalem desolata est.
      EN: The city of Your Holy One has become deserted; Zion has become a wilderness, Jerusalem desolate.

64:10  LA: Domus sanctificationis nostræ et gloriæ nostræ, ubi laudaverunt te patres nostri, facta est in exustionem ignis, et omnia desiderabilia nostra versa sunt in ruinas.
       EN: Our holy and glorious house, where our fathers praised You, has been burned with fire, and all our cherished things have been turned into ruins.

64:11  LA: Numquid super his continebis te, Domine ; tacebis, et affliges nos vehementer ?]
       EN: Will You restrain Yourself over these things, Lord? Will You remain silent and afflict us so severely?

ISAIAH 65

65:1  LA: Quæsierunt me qui ante non interrogabant ; invenerunt qui non quæsierunt me. Dixi : Ecce ego, ecce ego, ad gentem quæ non invocabat nomen meum.
      EN: Those who formerly did not inquire have sought Me; those who did not seek Me have found Me. I said, “Here I am, here I am,” to a nation that did not call upon My name.

65:2  LA: Expandi manus meas tota die ad populum incredulum, qui graditur in via non bona post cogitationes suas.
      EN: I have spread out My hands all day to an unbelieving people, who walk in a way that is not good, following their own thoughts.

65:3  LA: Populus qui ad iracundiam provocat me ante faciem meam semper ; qui immolant in hortis, et sacrificant super lateres ;
      EN: This people continually provokes Me to anger before My face: they sacrifice in gardens and offer sacrifices upon bricks;

65:4  LA: qui habitant in sepulchris, et in delubris idolorum dormiunt ; qui comedunt carnem suillam, et jus profanum in vasis eorum ;
      EN: they dwell in tombs and sleep in the shrines of idols; they eat pork, and unclean broth is in their vessels.

65:5  LA: qui dicunt : Recede a me, non appropinques mihi, quia immundus es. Isti fumus erunt in furore meo, ignis ardens tota die.
      EN: They say, “Keep away from me; do not approach me, for you are unclean.” These will be smoke in My fury, a fire burning all day.

65:6  LA: Ecce scriptum est coram me : Non tacebo, sed reddam, et retribuam in sinum eorum.
      EN: Look, it is written before Me: I will not be silent, but will repay; I will repay into their bosom

65:7  LA: Iniquitates vestras, et iniquitates patrum vestrorum simul, dicit Dominus ; qui sacrificaverunt super montes, et super colles exprobraverunt mihi ; et remetiar opus eorum primum in sinu eorum.
      EN: your iniquities and your fathers' iniquities together, says the Lord. They sacrificed upon mountains and taunted Me upon hills; I will first measure their deeds back into their bosom.

65:8  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Quomodo si inveniatur granum in botro, et dicatur : Ne dissipes illud, quoniam benedictio est : sic faciam propter servos meos, ut non disperdam totum.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Just as when a grape is found in a cluster and someone says, “Do not destroy it, for there is a blessing in it,” so I will act for My servants' sake, that I do not destroy everything.

65:9  LA: Et educam de Jacob semen, et de Juda possidentem montes meos ; et hæreditabunt eam electi mei, et servi mei habitabunt ibi.
      EN: I will bring offspring out of Jacob, and out of Judah one who will possess My mountains. My chosen ones will inherit the land, and My servants will dwell there.

65:10  LA: Et erunt campestria in caulas gregum, et vallis Achor in cubile armentorum, populo meo qui requisierunt me.
       EN: The plains will become folds for flocks, and the Valley of Achor a resting place for herds, for My people who have sought Me.

65:11  LA: Et vos qui dereliquistis Dominum, qui obliti estis montem sanctum meum, qui ponitis fortunæ mensam, et libatis super eam :
       EN: But you who have forsaken the Lord and forgotten My holy mountain, who set a table for Fortune and pour libations upon it,

65:12  LA: numerabo vos in gladio, et omnes in cæde corruetis : pro eo quod vocavi, et non respondistis ; locutus sum, et non audistis ; et faciebatis malum in oculis meis, et quæ nolui elegistis.
       EN: I will number you for the sword, and you will all fall in slaughter. For I called and you did not answer; I spoke and you did not hear. You did evil in My eyes and chose what I did not desire.

65:13  LA: Propter hoc hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce servi mei comedent, et vos esurietis ; ecce servi mei bibent, et vos sitietis ;
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Look, My servants will eat, but you will hunger. Look, My servants will drink, but you will thirst.

65:14  LA: ecce servi mei lætabuntur, et vos confundemini ; ecce servi mei laudabunt præ exsultatione cordis, et vos clamabitis præ dolore cordis, et præ contritione spiritus ululabitis,
       EN: Look, My servants will rejoice, but you will be ashamed. Look, My servants will sing praise from exultation of heart, but you will cry out from pain of heart and wail from a broken spirit.

65:15  LA: et dimittetis nomen vestrum in juramentum electis meis ; et interficiet te Dominus Deus, et servos suos vocabit nomine alio :
       EN: You will leave your name as an oath for My chosen ones. The Lord God will put you to death and call His servants by another name,

65:16  LA: in quo qui benedictus est super terram benedicetur in Deo, amen, et qui jurat in terra jurabit in Deo, amen : quia oblivioni traditæ sunt angustiæ priores, et quia absconditæ sunt ab oculis meis.
       EN: by which whoever is blessed upon the earth will be blessed in God, amen, and whoever swears upon the earth will swear by God, amen. For the former troubles have been given over to oblivion and hidden from My eyes.

65:17  LA: Ecce enim ego creo cælos novos, et terram novam ; et non erunt in memoria priora, et non ascendent super cor.
       EN: For look, I create new heavens and a new earth. The former things will not be remembered or come to mind.

65:18  LA: Sed gaudebitis et exsultabitis usque in sempiternum in his quæ ego creo : quia ecce ego creo Jerusalem exsultationem, et populum ejus gaudium.
       EN: But you will rejoice and exult forever in the things I create. For look, I create Jerusalem as exultation and her people as joy.

65:19  LA: Et exsultabo in Jerusalem, et gaudebo in populo meo, et non audietur in eo ultra vox fletus et vox clamoris.
       EN: I will exult in Jerusalem and rejoice in My people, and no longer will the sound of weeping or outcry be heard in it.

65:20  LA: Non erit ibi amplius infans dierum, et senex qui non impleat dies suos, quoniam puer centum annorum morietur, et peccator centum annorum maledictus erit.
       EN: There will no longer be an infant of only a few days or an old man who does not complete his days. For a child will die at a hundred years, and a sinner of a hundred years will be accursed.

65:21  LA: Et ædificabunt domos, et habitabunt ; et plantabunt vineas, et comedent fructus earum.
       EN: They will build houses and inhabit them; they will plant vineyards and eat their fruit.

65:22  LA: Non ædificabunt, et alius habitabit ; non plantabunt, et alius comedet : secundum enim dies ligni erunt dies populi mei, et opera manuum eorum inveterabunt.
       EN: They will not build for another to inhabit or plant for another to eat. For My people's days will be like the days of a tree, and the works of their hands will endure to old age.

65:23  LA: Electi mei non laborabunt frustra, neque generabunt in conturbatione, quia semen benedictorum Domini est, et nepotes eorum cum eis.
       EN: My chosen ones will not labor in vain or bear children for turmoil, for they are the offspring of those blessed by the Lord, and their descendants are with them.

65:24  LA: Eritque antequam clament, ego exaudiam ; adhuc illis loquentibus, ego audiam.
       EN: Before they cry out, I will answer; while they are still speaking, I will hear.

65:25  LA: Lupus et agnus pascentur simul, leo et bos comedent paleas, et serpenti pulvis panis ejus. Non nocebunt, neque occident in omni monte sancto meo, dicit Dominus.]
       EN: Wolf and lamb will feed together; lion and ox will eat straw, and dust will be the serpent's food. They will neither harm nor kill on all My holy mountain, says the Lord.

ISAIAH 66

66:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Cælum sedes mea, terra autem scabellum pedum meorum. Quæ est ista domus quam ædificabitis mihi ? et quis est iste locus quietis meæ ?
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Heaven is My throne, and earth the footstool of My feet. What is this house you will build for Me, and what is this place of My rest?

66:2  LA: Omnia hæc manus mea fecit, et facta sunt universa ista, dicit Dominus ; ad quem autem respiciam, nisi ad pauperculum, et contritum spiritu, et trementem sermones meos ?
      EN: My hand made all these things, and all these things came to be, says the Lord. But to whom will I look except the poor afflicted one, contrite in spirit, who trembles at My words?

66:3  LA: Qui immolat bovem, quasi qui interficiat virum ; qui mactat pecus, quasi qui excerebret canem ; qui offert oblationem, quasi qui sanguinem suillum offerat ; qui recordatur thuris, quasi qui benedicat idolo. Hæc omnia elegerunt in viis suis, et in abominationibus suis anima eorum delectata est.
      EN: The one who sacrifices an ox is like one who kills a man; the one who slaughters a sheep is like one who smashes a dog's skull; the one who presents an offering is like one who offers pig's blood; the one who makes a memorial offering of incense is like one who blesses an idol. They have chosen all these things in their ways, and their soul has delighted in their abominations.

66:4  LA: Unde et ego eligam illusiones eorum, et quæ timebant adducam eis ; quia vocavi, et non erat qui responderet ; locutus sum, et non audierunt ; feceruntque malum in oculis meis, et quæ nolui elegerunt.
      EN: Therefore I too will choose their delusions and bring upon them the things they feared. For I called and no one answered; I spoke and they did not hear. They did evil in My eyes and chose what I did not desire.

66:5  LA: Audite verbum Domini, qui tremitis ad verbum ejus. Dixerunt fratres vestri odientes vos, et abjicientes propter nomen meum : Glorificetur Dominus, et videbimus in lætitia vestra ; ipsi autem confundentur.
      EN: Hear the word of the Lord, you who tremble at His word. Your brothers who hate you and cast you out because of My name have said, “Let the Lord be glorified, and we will see your joy.” But they themselves will be ashamed.

66:6  LA: Vox populi de civitate, vox de templo, vox Domini reddentis retributionem inimicis suis.
      EN: A voice of the people from the city, a voice from the temple, the voice of the Lord repaying His enemies!

66:7  LA: Antequam parturiret, peperit ; antequam veniret partus ejus, peperit masculum.
      EN: Before she was in labor, she gave birth; before her birth pangs came, she bore a male child.

66:8  LA: Quis audivit umquam tale ? et quis vidit huic simile ? numquid parturiet terra in die una, aut parietur gens simul, quia parturivit et peperit Sion filios suos ?
      EN: Who has ever heard such a thing, or seen anything like it? Will the earth bring forth in one day, or a nation be born at once? For Zion was in labor and bore her children.

66:9  LA: Numquid ego qui alios parere facio, ipse non pariam ? dicit Dominus. Si ego, qui generationem ceteris tribuo, sterilis ero ? ait Dominus Deus tuus.
      EN: Shall I, who cause others to give birth, not Myself give birth? says the Lord. Shall I, who grant generation to others, be barren? says the Lord your God.

66:10  LA: Lætamini cum Jerusalem et exsultate in ea, omnes qui diligitis eam ; gaudete cum ea gaudio, universi qui lugetis super eam :
       EN: Rejoice with Jerusalem and exult in her, all who love her. Be glad with her in joy, all who mourn over her,

66:11  LA: ut sugatis et repleamini ab ubere consolationis ejus ; ut mulgeatis et deliciis affluatis ab omnimoda gloria ejus.
       EN: so that you may suck and be filled from her consoling breast, draw milk, and overflow with delights from the abundance of her glory.

66:12  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego declinabo super eam quasi fluvium pacis, et quasi torrentem inundantem gloriam gentium, quam sugetis : ad ubera portabimini, et super genua blandientur vobis.
       EN: For thus says the Lord: Look, I will direct over her a river of peace and the glory of nations like an overflowing torrent, from which you will suck. You will be carried at the breast and caressed upon the knees.

66:13  LA: Quomodo si cui mater blandiatur, ita ego consolabor vos, et in Jerusalem consolabimini.
       EN: As a mother caresses her child, so I will comfort you, and you will be comforted in Jerusalem.

66:14  LA: Videbitis, et gaudebit cor vestrum, et ossa vestra quasi herba germinabunt : et cognoscetur manus Domini servis ejus, et indignabitur inimicis suis.
       EN: You will see, and your heart will rejoice; your bones will flourish like grass. The Lord's hand will be known to His servants, and He will be indignant against His enemies.

66:15  LA: Quia ecce Dominus in igne veniet, et quasi turbo quadrigæ ejus, reddere in indignatione furorem suum et increpationem suam in flamma ignis :
       EN: For look, the Lord will come in fire, and His chariots like a whirlwind, to render His fury in indignation and His rebuke in flames of fire.

66:16  LA: quia in igne Dominus dijudicabit, et in gladio suo ad omnem carnem ; et multiplicabuntur interfecti a Domino,
       EN: For with fire the Lord will judge, and with His sword against all flesh; those slain by the Lord will be many,

66:17  LA: qui sanctificabantur et mundos se putabant in hortis post januam intrinsecus, qui comedebant carnem suillam, et abominationem et murem : simul consumentur, dicit Dominus.
       EN: those who consecrated themselves and considered themselves clean in the gardens, behind the door within, who ate pork, abominations, and mice. Together they will be consumed, says the Lord.

66:18  LA: Ego autem opera eorum et cogitationes eorum venio ut congregem, cum omnibus gentibus et linguis : et venient, et videbunt gloriam meam.
       EN: But I am coming to gather their works and thoughts, together with all nations and languages. They will come and see My glory.

66:19  LA: Et ponam in eis signum, et mittam ex eis qui salvati fuerint, ad gentes in mare, in Africam, et Lydiam, tendentes sagittam ; in Italiam et Græciam, ad insulas longe, ad eos qui non audierunt de me, et non viderunt gloriam meam. Et annuntiabunt gloriam meam gentibus ;
       EN: I will set a sign among them and send some who have been saved to the nations across the sea, to Africa and Lydia, whose people draw the bow, to Italy and Greece, to distant islands, to those who have not heard of Me or seen My glory. They will announce My glory to the nations.

66:20  LA: et adducent omnes fratres vestros de cunctis gentibus donum Domino, in equis, et in quadrigis, et in lecticis, et in mulis, et in carrucis, ad montem sanctum meum Jerusalem, dicit Dominus : quomodo si inferant filii Israël munus in vase mundo in domum Domini.
       EN: They will bring all your brothers from all nations as a gift to the Lord, on horses, in chariots and litters, on mules and in wagons, to My holy mountain Jerusalem, says the Lord, just as the children of Israel bring an offering in a clean vessel into the Lord's house.

66:21  LA: Et assumam ex eis in sacerdotes et Levitas, dicit Dominus.
       EN: I will also take some of them as priests and Levites, says the Lord.

66:22  LA: Quia sicut cæli novi et terra nova, quæ ego facio stare coram me, dicit Dominus, sic stabit semen vestrum et nomen vestrum.
       EN: For as the new heavens and the new earth that I make stand before Me, says the Lord, so your offspring and your name will stand.

66:23  LA: Et erit mensis ex mense, et sabbatum ex sabbato : veniet omnis caro ut adoret coram facie mea, dicit Dominus.
       EN: From month to month and from Sabbath to Sabbath, all flesh will come to worship before Me, says the Lord.

66:24  LA: Et egredientur, et videbunt cadavera virorum qui prævaricati sunt in me ; vermis eorum non morietur, et ignis eorum non extinguetur : et erunt usque ad satietatem visionis omni carni.]
       EN: They will go out and see the corpses of the men who transgressed against Me. Their worm will not die, and their fire will not be extinguished; they will be a sight that all flesh sees to satiety.
